### [1] [RISE: Adaptive Imagination for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20430)
*Hongbo Lu,Liang Yao,Chenghao He,Hao Han,Fan Liu,Wenlong Liao,Tao He,Pai Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RISE框架，通过自适应想象预算分配和CounterDrive数据集，解决现有世界动作模型（WAMs）固定想象预算导致的效率低下问题，提升规划性能并减少冗余计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在动作生成中纳入未来世界演化以改进规划，但所有场景均分配固定的想象预算，导致计算资源浪费或规划不足。此外，真实驾驶日志仅展示单一实现的未来，缺乏对多样化风险和后果的覆盖，限制了模型对风险的学习能力。

Method: 1. 构建RISE系统级自适应想象框架：基于预期规划收益进行顺序的Roll/Stop决策。包含Latent Evaluator评估当前前缀的风险及继续想象的潜在提升，以及Rollout Gate权衡预期收益与计算成本。2. 构建CounterDrive反事实数据集：提供多样化结果和风险水平的数据，用于丰富未来动态并提供局部风险监督。样本经专家验证标注轨迹有效性、事故 onset 和因果类别。

Result: 在NAVSIM和nuScenes基准测试中，RISE实现了最佳的总体规划性能，同时显著减少了不必要的rollout步骤。额外的迁移实验表明该方法具有插件般的通用性，可适配不同的WAM架构。

Conclusion: RISE通过自适应调整想象深度有效平衡了计算成本与规划质量，而CounterDrive数据集为安全关键的世界建模研究提供了宝贵的 reusable 资源，二者结合提升了自动驾驶规划的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve planning by incorporating future world evolution into action generation, yet existing methods allocate a fixed imagination budget to every scene. We propose RISE (\textbf{R}efining \textbf{I}magination through \textbf{SE}lective Rollout), a system-level adaptive imagination framework that makes sequential \textsc{Roll}/\textsc{Stop} decisions according to the expected planning benefit of continued rollout. At each step, a Latent Evaluator estimates the risk revealed by the current prefix and how much planning could improve if imagination continues, while a Rollout Gate weighs this expected benefit against additional computation cost. Since factual driving logs expose only one realized future, we further construct \textbf{CounterDrive}, a counterfactual dataset with diverse outcomes and risk levels, to enrich future dynamics and provide localized risk supervision. Each retained sample undergoes expert verification and annotation of trajectory validity, incident onset, and causal category, providing a reusable resource for safety-critical world-modeling research. Experiments on NAVSIM and nuScenes show that RISE achieves the best overall planning performance while reducing unnecessary rollout, with additional transfer results supporting its plug-in generality across WAM architectures.

</details>


### [2] [Aggregating Visual Information with Optimal Transport for VideoLM Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20473)
*Wenti Yin,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AVIOT方法，利用最优传输理论压缩视频视觉Token，通过问题条件调节和空间粒度自适应融合，在保持高压缩比的同时实现高效的视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型处理视频时产生大量具有代表性的冗余视觉Token，导致解码负担过重；核心挑战在于如何在压缩过程中保留分散在各帧中的关键视觉信息。

Method: 引入聚合视觉信息与最优传输（AVIOT）框架，将视频Token压缩建模为将密集的经验分布映射到紧凑目标分布的最优传输问题。具体包括：1）通过问题条件（Question conditioning）调制源帧与目标支持点之间的传输成本，动态分配时间段的表示容量以聚焦相关问题内容；2）在多空间粒度上计算区域特定的时间传输计划，并自适应融合不同时刻的表征，使紧凑表示能整合不同时间片段的信息。

Result: 在不同压缩比率下的评估显示，AVIOT在多个视频理解基准测试中匹配或优于未压缩基线，并在高压缩比下仍保持强劲性能。

Conclusion: AVIOT通过最优传输有效解决了视频Token压缩中的信息保留难题，实现了高效且高质量的视频内容表征，提升了长视频处理的效率与准确性。

Abstract: Video language models process videos as dense visual-token sequences with substantial representational redundancy. Compressing these sequences is therefore essential for reducing the visual-token burden on language-model decoding. The central challenge is to preserve visual information dispersed across frames under such compression. To this end, we introduce Aggregating Visual Information with Optimal Transport (AVIOT), which casts video token compression as transporting a dense empirical measure of frame observations onto a compact target measure. The resulting source-to-target coupling induces a distribution over source observations for each target support, directly specifying how the compressed video representation is constructed. We further adapt this construction along task and spatial axes. Question conditioning modulates the transport cost between source frames and target supports, while influencing how many supports are allocated to each temporal segment, thereby directing representation capacity toward question-relevant content. At multiple spatial granularities, AVIOT computes region-specific temporal transport plans and adaptively fuses the representations they yield, allowing different regions within the same compact representation to draw from different moments. Evaluations across varying compression ratios show that AVIOT matches or outperforms the uncompressed baseline on multiple video-understanding benchmarks while retaining strong performance at higher compression ratios.

</details>


### [3] [Annotations as Rollouts: Efficient and Scalable Reinforcement Learning for Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20492)
*Yunheng Li,Guohong Mu,Hao Li,Shengsheng Qian,Dingwen Zhang,Qibin Hou,Ming-Ming Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OraRL，一种针对视频多模态大模型（MLLMs）的高效强化学习后训练方法。通过引入“Oracle rollout”作为直接优化目标，并设计解耦的优势估计器解决优势反转问题，显著提升了样本效率和推理速度，在多个视频理解基准上超越了现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习（RL）后训练方法在大规模多任务数据集上面临挑战，主要因为采样策略组内高质量轨迹（rollouts）稀少，且依赖昂贵的思维链（CoT）生成，导致样本效率低、扩展性差。

Method: 1. **Oracle Rollout集成**：将标注数据不仅用于评分，还作为策略组中的“Oracle rollout”直接作为正向优化目标。
2. **解耦优势估计器（Decoupled Advantage Estimator）**：解决高奖励Oracle导致的“优势反转”问题。策略轨迹决定无Oracle基线，Oracle-策略差距调制方向增益和独立的分离式Oracle优势。
3. **符号平衡剪枝（Sign-balanced pruning）**：仅保留每个符号方向最强的Oracle和轨迹，提升效率。

Result: 1. **效率提升**：OraRL仅需SFT 2.2倍步时间，低于GRPO+CoT的4.9倍；无需Co时，Video-ORA-9B解码时间为130ms（对比4,780ms）。
2. **性能超越**：Temporal mIoU从62.5提升至66.0，Tracking AO从73.0提升至78.2，Segmentation从64.3提升至70.4，三维基准空间智能宏观平均值从51.0提升至56.1。
3. **基准测试**：在VSI-Bench上得分73.1，优于GPT-5 (55.0) 和 Gemini-3-Pro (55.1)。
4. **可扩展性**：模型规模从0.8B扩展至9B，数据量达100k prompts时仍有效超越GRPO。

Conclusion: OraRL通过创新性地利用标注数据作为Oracle rollout并采用解耦优势估计机制，解决了RL后训练中的样本效率和优势反转难题，实现了高效、可扩展的视频多模态大模型训练，并在多项指标上达到当前最佳水平。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have become a prevailing paradigm for unified video perception. However, post-training on large multi-task datasets remains challenging, as existing reinforcement learning methods sample on-policy groups with few high-quality rollouts even with costly chain-of-thought (CoT) generation. In this paper, we study the sample efficiency and scalability of RL post-training for video MLLMs and introduce OraRL. We identify an overlooked role for annotations: Beyond scoring rollouts, each can enter its on-policy group as an oracle rollout, a direct positive optimization target. Direct oracle integration, however, is nontrivial: a high-reward oracle raises the group baseline and inverts otherwise positive policy advantages, a failure we term advantage inversion. At the core of OraRL is a decoupled advantage estimator: policy rollouts determine an oracle-free baseline, while the oracle-policy gap modulates both a directional gain and a separate detached oracle advantage. Sign-balanced pruning improves efficiency: by retaining only the oracle and the strongest rollouts of each sign, OraRL requires just 2.2x the step time of SFT, less than half the 4.9x required by GRPO with CoT. OraRL scales with model size and data, surpassing its backbone from 0.8B to 9B and GRPO up to 100k prompts. Without chain-of-thought, Video-ORA-9B decodes in 130 ms instead of 4,780 ms. Compared with the respective prior best models, it raises temporal mIoU from 62.5 to 66.0, tracking AO from 73.0 to 78.2, segmentation from 64.3 to 70.4, and the three-benchmark spatial-intelligence macro average from 51.0 to 56.1; on VSI-Bench, it scores 73.1 against 55.0 for GPT-5 and 55.1 for Gemini-3-Pro.

</details>


### [4] [DiffVC-ONE: Diffusion-based Generative Video Compression with One-Step Video Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20515)
*Wenzhuo Ma,Zhenzhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffVC-ONE，一种基于单步视频扩散Transformer的生成式视频压缩框架，旨在同时实现高时间一致性、丰富视觉细节恢复和低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式视频压缩虽能在低码率下恢复丰富视觉细节，但难以同时兼顾高时间一致性和低推理成本。

Method: 1. 统一单向潜在压缩器：使用共享模型高效均匀地压缩紧凑潜在切片；2. 基于Video DiT的单步扩散增强器：以重构潜在切片为内容锚点，对整组图像进行单步时空感知增强；3. 混合条件生成器：从重构内容和量化信息中提取结构、强度和语义条件，以保留忠实区域、控制增强程度并补充感知细节。

Result: 在多个标准基准测试中，DiffVC-ONE实现了最先进的感知质量和时间一致性，同时保持了较低的推理成本。

Conclusion: 该框架通过单步扩散机制和混合条件引导，有效解决了生成式视频压缩中质量与效率难以平衡的问题。

Abstract: Generative video compression can recover rich visual details at low bitrates, but simultaneously achieving high temporal consistency and low inference cost remains challenging. To address this issue, we propose DiffVC-ONE, a diffusion-based generative video compression framework built on a one-step Video Diffusion Transformer. First, we introduce a Unified Unidirectional Latent Compressor that uses a shared model to efficiently and uniformly compress compact latent slices. We then develop a Video DiT-based One-Step Diffusion Enhancer that uses the reconstructed latent slices as content anchors and performs single-step spatio-temporal perceptual enhancement over an entire group of pictures. Finally, a Hybrid Condition Generator extracts structural, strength, and semantic conditions from the reconstructed content and quantization information. These conditions preserve faithful regions, control the degree of generative enhancement, and supplement content-aware perceptual details during one-step diffusion enhancement. Extensive experiments on multiple standard benchmarks demonstrate that DiffVC-ONE achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality and temporal consistency with low inference cost.

</details>


### [5] [Grounded-Exo2Ego: Structured Semantic Grounding for Robust Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20534)
*Shengze Wang,Michael Stengel,Tianye Li,Seonwook Park,Amrita Mazumdar,Koki Nagano,Alex Trevithick,Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Grounded-Exo2Ego框架，通过几何锚定与语义接地双分支扩散模型及相机重定位算法，解决从外视角到内视角视频生成的难题，在EgoExo4D数据集上显著优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外视角到内视角（exo-to-ego）视频生成对于AR/VR和物理AI至关重要，但因极端视角变化和不可见区域导致传统几何条件失效，任务难度远高于常规新视图合成。

Method: 1. 架构：双分支视频扩散模型，结合3D重建渲染的几何锚定分支与基于对象级上下文的语义接地分支；2. 数据：引入相机重定位算法解决相机-重建不对齐问题；3. 数据引擎：开发全自动合成数据引擎，生成程序化环境中的 rigged 3D角色。

Result: 在具有挑战性的EgoExo4D数据集上，该方法在所有指标上大幅超越最新最先进方法；消融实验验证了数据和架构层面各贡献的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过整合几何与语义信息、解决相机对齐问题以及利用自动化合成数据，有效克服了外视角到内视角生成的核心挑战，实现了高质量的视频生成。

Abstract: Generating egocentric video from a single exocentric video is an emerging and important topic for AR/VR and physical AI. Compared with conventional novel view synthesis, exo-to-ego generation is a significantly harder task because the standard geometric conditioning becomes highly unreliable under extreme view changes and large unobservable regions. We present Grounded-Exo2Ego, a principled framework that addresses these challenges at both the architectural and data levels. Architecturally, Grounded-Exo2Ego is a dual-branch video diffusion model that couples a geometric anchoring branch, which conditions the generation on the rendering of a 3D reconstruction, with a novel semantic grounding branch, which goes beyond the prevailing geometry-based approach and improves quality by synthesizing challenging regions based on object-level context. Additionally, we found that the overlooked issue of camera-reconstruction misalignment severely undermines exo-to-ego learning. We thus introduce a camera re-localization algorithm that resolves this issue and substantially improves quality across all metrics. We further develop a fully automated synthetic data engine that generates and renders rigged 3D characters in procedurally generated environments. Evaluation on the challenging EgoExo4D dataset shows that our method outperforms recent state-of-the-art approaches by large margins across all metrics. Detailed ablations validate improvements from each of our contributions at both the data and architectural level.

</details>


### [6] [Keep Your Friends Close, and the Right Neighbours Closer: Disaster-Conditioned Kernel-Regularized Graph Attention for Building Damage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20548)
*Fuad Hasan,Chul Min Yeum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种灾害类型感知的图注意力模型，通过自适应邻域尺度和残差去相关损失，解决空间上下文在灾害损伤分类中容易过度平滑和错误传播的问题，提升了零样本事件迁移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灾害损伤分类主要依赖单体建筑外观特征，忽视了空间上下文；而简单的空间聚合会导致边界模糊或结构化误差传播。不同灾害（洪水、飓风、野火）具有不同的聚类行为，需要适应性的空间推理而非单一的平滑规则。

Method: 1. 基于GPS构建建筑图表示空间关系；2. 引入灾害类型条件化的图模型，注入可学习的多尺度空间核先验到注意力机制，使有效邻域尺度随灾害类型自适应调整；3. 设计残差去相关损失函数，惩罚预测残差中的正Moran's I值，防止通过过度平滑获得虚假的空间一致性。

Result: 在xBD数据集上采用留一事件交叉验证（LOEO协议），并在从xBD到Ida-BD的数据集间迁移测试中，该方法显著提高了宏观F1分数，并大幅降低了残差的空间自相关性，证明了其能更有效地利用空间上下文而非简单平滑。

Conclusion: 通过灾害类型条件化的自适应空间建模和去相关正则化，可以有效利用空间上下文信息，避免 naive smoothing 带来的负面影响，从而提升模型在面对未知灾害事件时的泛化和迁移能力。

Abstract: Disaster damage is spatial: buildings rarely fail in isolation. Yet using spatial context for damage classification remains surprisingly underexplored, and many pipelines still rely primarily on per-building appearance cues even when the dominant uncertainty is spatially structured. Complicating matters, the right neighbourhood is not the same across events. Floods, hurricanes, and wildfires can exhibit very different clustering behaviour, making spatial reasoning valuable but easy to misuse - naive context aggregation can improve visual coherence while oversmoothing boundaries or propagating structured errors. We study this tension on xBD (the dataset used in the xView2 challenge) in a controlled post-localization, classification-only setup: each building is represented by a pre/post combined (PPC) patch cropped from the provided polygons, and spatial context is modelled with GPS-derived building graphs. Our approach keeps local evidence "close" by preserving strong spatial relationships in disaster damage patterns, while bringing only the right neighbours "closer" through a disaster-type-conditioned graph model that injects a learnable multi-scale spatial kernel prior into attention, allowing the effective neighbourhood scale to adapt across disaster types rather than being learned as a single global smoothing rule. To discourage coherence-by-smoothing, we add a residual de-correlation loss that penalizes positive Moran's~I in prediction residuals. We evaluate the method under event and dataset shift with a leave-one-event-out (LOEO) protocol on xBD and cross-dataset transfer from xBD to Ida-BD. The model improves macro-F1 and substantially reduces residual spatial autocorrelation under zero-shot event shift, indicating better use of spatial context rather than naive smoothing and enabling more reliable transfer to unseen events within known disaster types.

</details>


### [7] [Learning Prostate Anatomy at Test Time for Cancer Detection in Micro-Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20557)
*Obed Korshie Dzikunu,Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Mohamed Harmanani,Paul F. R. Wilson,Emma Willis,Ferdinand Luger,Adam Kinnaird,Brian Wodlinger,Parvin Mousavi,Purang Abolmaesumi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ANT框架，通过测试时辅助前列腺分割任务对齐解剖结构，解决微超声图像中的域偏移问题，显著提升前列腺癌检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时适应（TTA）方法仅校正图像外观的统计差异，忽略目标域的解剖结构，导致在临床中心间因成像硬件或协议不同而产生的域偏移问题未能根本解决，阻碍深度学习模型在前列腺癌检测中的部署。

Method: 提出ANT（Segmentation-guided TTA），在测试时利用冻结的预训练分割网络生成的伪掩码监督辅助前列腺分割任务。通过使编码器表示与目标域的前列腺解剖结构对齐，修正特定于域的特征漂移，同时保留癌症判别性结构信息。

Result: 在多中心临床试验数据上评估：使用早期微超声扫描仪训练的693名患者模型，在两个新中心使用新一代系统采集的118名患者上进行留一中心交叉验证。相比无适应方法，ANT在活检核心层面和患者层面的平均AUC分别提升了2.9%和3.6%，优于现有TTA基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入解剖结构感知的测试时适应机制，ANT能有效缓解跨临床中心的域偏移问题，提升前列腺癌检测模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性，为临床部署提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain shift across clinical centers using different imaging hardware or acquisition protocols remains a fundamental barrier to deploying deep learning models for prostate cancer (PCa) detection. Existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods address distribution shift through entropy minimization or augmentation-based self-supervision, correcting for statistical differences in image appearance but ignoring the anatomical structure of the target domain. We propose ANT, a segmentation-guided TTA framework that adapts a pretrained cancer detection encoder to the target domain by solving an auxiliary prostate segmentation task at test time, supervised by pseudo-masks from a frozen pretrained segmentation network. By aligning encoder representations to prostate anatomy in the target domain, ANT corrects domain-specific feature drift while preserving cancer-discriminative structure. The model was trained on 693 patients imaged with an earlier-generation micro-ultrasound scanner in a multi-center clinical trial, and evaluated on 118 patients acquired with a newer-generation system across two centers in another clinical trial. Under a leave-one-center-out protocol with identical evaluation conditions across all methods, ANT improves mean AUC by 2.9% and 3.6% at the biopsy-core and patient levels, respectively, over no adaptation, outperforming TTA baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/ObedDzik/ant.git.

</details>


### [8] [Zero-Shot Color Image Manipulation Localization via Noise Residual Artifact Pattern Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20558)
*Edgar Gonzalez-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、零样本的盲图像篡改定位方法，通过单张可疑图像的噪声残差估计参考伪影模式，利用块级相关性和高斯混合模型生成像素级篡改概率图，克服了现有方法依赖特定通道或训练数据的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动取证方法主要依赖拜耳残差的绿色通道，丢弃了其他颜色通道的关联信息，且通常需要训练数据或设备注册，限制了其通用性和零样本应用场景。

Method: 构建了一个零样本、无训练的盲图像篡改定位流水线：1) 基于获取到插值噪声方差比原则性地选择去噪器；2) 从单张图像的噪声残差中直接估计参考伪影模式；3) 对估计的参考模式进行块级相关性分析；4) 使用双分量高斯混合模型评分阶段，生成像素级篡改概率图。

Result: 消融实验评估了去噪器选择和块大小对定位准确性的影响；与最先进被动方法的比较表明，所提出的零样本方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效利用全色通道的信息，无需预先训练或设备注册，即可实现有效的图像真实性评估和篡改定位。

Abstract: Digital cameras embed device-specific artifacts into every acquired image through demosaicing, in-camera post-processing, and lossy compression. These traces constitute a forensic signal that can be exploited to assess image authenticity. Existing passive methods rely predominantly on the green channel of the Bayer residual, discarding the correlated information available in the remaining color channels and typically requiring training data or device enrollment. This work proposes a zero-shot, training-free blind image manipulation localization pipeline that estimates a reference artifact pattern directly from the noise residual of a single suspect image, without assuming a fixed filter configuration, color layout, or block period. The pipeline incorporates a principled denoiser selection criterion based on the acquired-to-interpolated noise variance ratio, a block-level correlation analysis against the estimated reference pattern, and a two-component Gaussian Mixture Model scoring stage that produces a pixel-level tampering probability map. An ablation study evaluates the impact of denoiser choice and block size on localization accuracy, and comparisons against state-of-the-art passive methods demonstrate the competitiveness of the proposed zero-shot approach.

</details>


### [9] [Aggregate, Don't Adapt: Subject-Level Posterior Aggregation and Transductive Calibration for Cross-Site Parkinsonian Gait Severity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20587)
*Junlong Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在MoCha 2026帕金森步态基准赛中获得第一名的解决方案。该方法仅使用冻结的公开运动编码器配合单层线性层，通过精确复现参考基准流程、受试者级别的平均后验概率聚合以及无标签转导校准等“记账式”步骤，实现了0.6945的宏观F1分数，显著优于第二名和基线。研究指出微调编码器效果不佳，且内部交叉验证与最终得分负相关，强调了受试者级聚合的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决从临床未见过站点记录的标准化SMPL动作数据中预测MDS-UPDRS步态严重程度的问题，并在MoCha 2026基准挑战赛中获得优异表现。

Method: 采用冻结的公开运动编码器，仅附加一个$4\times512$的线性层进行分类。关键策略包括：1) 严格复现参考基准的头文件配方；2) 对组织者提供的受试者分组内的每段行走后验概率进行平均；3) 对特征均值和决策操作点进行无标签转导校准。

Result: 系统在隐藏测试集上达到0.6945的宏观F1分数，在58个参赛作品中排名第一。该成绩领先第二名（0.5807）和主办方基线（0.4289）显著幅度。消融实验显示微调编码器会导致性能下降，其他替代编码器表现更差。

Conclusion: 主要的性能提升来源于数据处理和后处理细节（特别是受试者级聚合），而非模型结构的复杂化或参数微调。内部留一队列交叉验证与最终隐藏测试得分呈负相关，揭示了评估指标的特性。受试者级聚合被确定为该基准的主要性能上限因素。

Abstract: We describe the winning entry to the MoCha 2026 Benchmark and Challenge on Parkinsonian Gait, which predicts MDS-UPDRS gait severity from canonicalized SMPL motion recorded at clinical sites unseen during training. The system reaches 0.6945 macro-F1 on the hidden test and ranked first of 58 entries, ahead of the runner-up at 0.5807 and the organizers' baseline at 0.4289, on a frozen public motion encoder with a single $4\times512$ linear layer. Nearly all of the margin comes from three stages usually treated as bookkeeping: reproducing the reference benchmark's exact head recipe, averaging per-walk posteriors within the subject grouping the organizers ship, and a label-free transductive calibration of the feature mean and the decision operating point. Fine-tuning the encoder lost in four distinct forms, and ten alternative encoders were worse. Every ablation number is a paid read on the hidden test, because our own leave-two-cohort-out cross-validation proved anti-correlated with the deciding score over eleven configurations. We give the negative record in full, and identify our largest gain, subject-level aggregation, as the binding ceiling on this benchmark.

</details>


### [10] [A Dataset-Centric Benchmark of Deep Learning Methods for Grape Leaf Disease Classification and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20608)
*Petar Canoski,Vlatko Spasev,Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Petre Lameski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了葡萄叶病害识别的深度学习基准测试，揭示了现有模型在真实复杂场景和跨数据集迁移中性能显著下降的问题，强调了数据集来源、现场评估及标注兼容性对可靠识别的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在葡萄叶病害识别中表现优异，但多数研究依赖少量受控条件下采集的数据集，难以反映真实果园中复杂的背景、光照变化、遮挡、叶片姿态及设备差异等挑战，导致模型泛化能力不足。

Method: 1. 分析公开数据集的疾病类别、标注类型、采集条件、图像特征、类别分布及任务适用性；2. 在图像级分类、区域级分类和目标检测三种设置下评估代表性模型；3. 使用准确率评估分类，mAP@50和mAP@50:95评估检测；4. 进行跨数据集实验以检验具有兼容疾病类别但视觉和标注特征不同的数据集之间的迁移能力。

Result: 1. 在多个受控或衍生数据集上，分类性能接近饱和；2. 在异构数据集上分类难度更大；3. 不同标注设置下的检测性能存在巨大差异；4. 跨数据集性能急剧下降，尤其是目标检测，表明共享疾病标签并不等同于等效的识别任务。

Conclusion: 可靠的葡萄园病害识别应强调数据集来源的可追溯性、真实的田间评估、标注的兼容性以及外部验证，而非仅关注单一数据集上的高性能。

Abstract: Grape leaf disease recognition is important for precision agriculture, enabling early diagnosis, timely intervention, and improved vineyard management. Although deep learning has achieved strong results, many studies rely on few datasets, often acquired under controlled conditions, and may not reflect real vineyard challenges such as complex backgrounds, variable illumination, occlusion, leaf pose, disease severity, and device differences. This paper presents a dataset-centric benchmark of deep learning methods for grape leaf disease classification and detection. We analyze publicly available datasets in terms of disease categories, annotation types, acquisition conditions, image characteristics, class distributions, provenance, and task suitability. Representative models are evaluated in three settings: image-level classification, region-level classification, and object detection. Classification is assessed using accuracy, while detection is evaluated using mAP@50 and mAP@50:95. Cross-dataset experiments further examine transfer between datasets with compatible disease categories but different visual and annotation characteristics. Results show near-saturated classification performance on several controlled or derivative datasets, greater difficulty on heterogeneous datasets, and substantial variation in detection performance across annotation settings. Cross-dataset performance drops sharply, especially for object detection, indicating that shared disease labels do not necessarily define equivalent recognition tasks. The benchmark emphasizes dataset provenance, realistic field evaluation, annotation compatibility, and external validation for reliable vineyard disease recognition.

</details>


### [11] [RECOUNT: Reference-guided Counting with Synthetic Visual Exemplars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20621)
*Adriano D'Alessandro,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Ghassan Hamarneh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RECOUNT框架，通过单张参考图像结合扩散模型生成的多样化样本库，解决文本引导零样本计数在细粒度类别上分类能力弱的问题，显著降低MAE误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导的零样本计数器在空间定位上表现优异，但在处理新颖或细粒度类别时，因自然语言描述过于粗略而无法区分视觉相似的干扰物；少样本计数器虽能避免此问题，但需对每张图像进行手动标注，成本高昂。

Method: 引入RECOUNT插件式框架：1) 使用单张离屏参考图像而非文本提示来指定类别；2) 利用去噪扩散概率模型（Diffusion Model）作为自动化对比数据引擎，将单一参考图扩展为多样化的示例图库以覆盖更广泛的类别外观；3) 采用冻结的计数器生成类别无关提议，并将分类任务卸载给一个独立的视觉模块（冻结主干网络+轻量级头部），该头部在合成数据上训练，用于匹配目标与干扰物图库。

Result: 在LookAlikes基准上将计数误差（MAE）降低了55%，在PairTally基准上降低了21%，优于最强的先前零样本计数器，实现了最佳的零样本准确率。

Conclusion: 通过视觉引导和扩散模型增强数据多样性，RECOUNT有效解决了文本语义模糊导致的细粒度分类难题，无需额外标注即可显著提升零样本计数的准确性。

Abstract: Text-guided zero-shot object counters excel at spatial localization but categorize poorly on novel or fine-grained classes: natural language is too coarse to fully specify visual identity, so they fail to separate visually similar distractors. Few-shot counters sidestep this with visual exemplars, but require manual annotations on every image. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce RECOUNT, a plug-and-play framework for image-guided zero-shot counting. Rather than specify a category with a text prompt, our key insight is to specify it visually, from a single off-scene reference image. However, we find that a lone reference image provides narrow coverage of a category's appearance and is unreliable across diverse scenes. We therefore repurpose a diffusion model as an automated contrastive data engine that expands the reference into a diverse exemplar gallery, supplying the discriminative detail that text cannot. RECOUNT preserves the class-agnostic proposals of any frozen counter and offloads categorization to a separate visual module (a frozen backbone with a lightweight head trained on this synthetic data) that matches each proposal against the target and distractor galleries. Applied to a frozen counter, RECOUNT attains the best zero-shot accuracy on both benchmarks, cutting counting error (MAE) by 55% on LookAlikes and 21% on PairTally relative to the strongest prior zero-shot counter.

</details>


### [12] [MV2GF: Multi-view Pedestrian Detection with a Visual Geometric Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20639)
*Taiga Yamane,Satoshi Suzuki,Ryo Masumura,Shota Orihashi,Tomohiro Tanaka,Mana Ihori,Naoki Makishima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MV2GF，利用视觉几何基础模型解决多视图行人检测（MVPD）在未见相机配置下的泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MVPD方法在处理未见相机配置时存在两大缺陷：难以捕捉跨视图的准确视觉几何，且模型过度依赖训练中的图像特征投影畸变模式。

Method: 引入具有强泛化能力的视觉几何基础模型（MV2GF），通过融合任务特定特征与通用几何特征来捕捉视觉几何；利用基础模型预测的3D点图将图像像素投影到准确的3D位置，避免对训练畸变的依赖。

Result: 实验证明，利用视觉几何基础模型进行MVPD是有效的，且MV2GF相比现有方法具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于视觉几何基础模型的方法能显著提升多视图行人检测在未知相机配置下的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Abstract: Multi-View Pedestrian Detection (MVPD) aims to detect pedestrians in the form of a bird's eye view map from multi-view images. Recent MVPD methods adopt a unified framework that projects 2D image features into a 3D world space and aggregates them into a single feature. Although they are effective, they struggle to generalize to unseen camera configurations during training due to two main issues. First, they are difficult to capture accurate visual geometry across views in unseen camera configurations. Second, they make detection models highly dependent on distortion patterns during training arising from their image feature projection. To address these, we leverage a visual geometric foundation model and propose MV2GF. This foundation model has exhibited strong generalization in capturing visual geometry across views and predicting accurate 3D attributes in diverse camera configurations. MV2GF fuses task-specific features with general-purpose geometric features extracted by the foundation model to effectively capture the visual geometry even in unseen camera configurations. Furthermore, MV2GF projects each pixel in the image features to an appropriate 3D location using 3D pointmaps predicted by the foundation model, preventing the detection model from depending on distortion patterns during training. Our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of leveraging a visual geometric foundation model for MVPD and that MV2GF generalizes better than existing methods.

</details>


### [13] [Lift, Associate, and Fuse: A Decision-Centric Framework for 2D-to-3D Foundation Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20659)
*Wentao Sun,Yiping Chen,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LAF框架，通过五个算子（生成、关联、协调、融合、持久化/查询）将2D到3D分割的转移过程标准化，解决现有方法因任务或表示分组而掩盖的关键决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的2D到3D分割转移方法通常按任务或数据表示分类，这种分类方式模糊了决定系统跨视图一致性的关键决策（如证据锚定点、身份识别时机、冲突处理等），导致难以系统性地比较和诊断不同系统的性能与缺陷。

Method: 引入“提升、关联与融合（LAF）”决策中心框架，将转移系统定义为五个操作算子；定义持久载体的显式契约（空间支持、语义状态、身份状态、不确定性、来源及支持操作）；将其操作化为结构化审计协议，并对161个系统进行评估，包括点、场、高斯、对象、图及基于记忆的载体。

Result: 通过对161个系统的审计，揭示了四个 recurring properties：关联不确立身份；载体设计固定了查询接口和校正边界；渲染视图、原生3D和提议级评估不可互换；以及诸如“免训练”、“实时”等限定词必须结合具体阶段和完整成本账本才有意义。

Conclusion: LAF提供了一种表示无关的方法，用于比较现有系统、诊断不可逆故障，并为未来智能体指定可修订的3D感知能力，强调了在特定阶段和成本背景下评估模型属性的必要性。

Abstract: Methods that transfer predictions from two-dimensional foundation models into three-dimensional segmentation are commonly grouped by task or representation. Those groupings obscure the decisions that determine whether a system remains coherent across views: where image evidence is grounded, when observations become one identity, how semantic and granularity conflicts are handled, which information is fused, and what state survives for later queries. We introduce \textbf{Lift, Associate, and Fuse (LAF)}, a decision-centric framework that represents a transfer system as five operators: \textbf{Generate, Associate, Reconcile, Fuse, and Persist/Query}. LAF defines an explicit contract for the persistent carrier---its spatial support, semantic state, identity state, uncertainty, provenance, and supported operations---and identifies the first stage at which discarded evidence becomes unrecoverable. We operationalize the framework as a structured audit protocol and apply it to 161 systems available through 7 August 2026, spanning point-, field-, Gaussian-, object-, graph-, and memory-based carriers. Representation, temporal, relational, and feed-forward stress tests required no additional analytical stage after the final confirmation pass. The resulting decision traces expose four recurring properties: association does not establish identity; carrier design fixes both the query interface and correction boundary; rendered-view, native-3D, and proposal-level evaluations are not interchangeable; and qualifiers such as \emph{training-free}, \emph{real-time}, \emph{open-vocabulary}, and \emph{generalizable} are meaningful only when attached to a stage and a complete cost ledger. LAF therefore supplies a representation-neutral method for comparing existing systems, diagnosing irreversible failures, and specifying revisable 3D perception for future agents.

</details>


### [14] [Shortcut Learning in a Public Grape Disease Dataset: Annotation Granularity as a Modulator, Not a Cause](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20663)
*Pushuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现农业病害检测数据集中，标注粒度（整体叶片vs病灶水平）显著影响模型跨物种泛化能力中的假阳性率。粗粒度标注会放大“捷径学习”效应，导致非目标物种图像中出现大量误检；细粒度标注则抑制该效应。标注粒度是调节因子而非根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共农业病害数据集通常仅依据报告指标判断可用性，忽视了标注方案内部的一致性。特别是不同类别在标注粒度上的不一致可能引入偏差，影响模型真实泛化能力评估，而这一问题在分布内评估中往往不可见。

Method: 1. 分析葡萄病害数据集（3288张图像，6个类别），比较不同模型容量、输入分辨率和检测范式下的性能表现。
2. 使用5156张不含葡萄的跨物种图像测试假阳性情况。
3. 通过反事实重训练实验：缩小粗粒度类别的边界框面积，观察跨物种假阳性变化。
4. 进行反向操作控制实验：将最细粒度类别统一为整体叶片级别，验证其是否产生新的假阳性。
5. 提出一种无需图像和训练的粒度筛选统计量。

Result: 1. 模型性能差异主要源于数据侧问题：一个类别以整体叶片级别标注（占图像面积43.16%），其余五个以病灶级别标注。
2. 跨物种图像中65.7%的假阳性集中在粗粒度类别，其过代表程度达13.41倍。
3. 缩小粗粒度类别边界框后，其跨物种假阳性减少66%，证明粒度可调节捷径效应强度。
4. 将细粒度类别粗化为整体叶片级别后，并未产生新的假阳性，说明粒度不能创造捷径，只能放大或减弱已存在的缺陷。
5. 提出的粒度筛选统计量有效，且发现空气传播病灶级检测在光学上难以实现。

Conclusion: 标注粒度是影响模型跨物种泛化中假阳性的关键调节因素，但并非根本成因。当前分布内评估无法揭示此类失败模式，需关注标注一致性与粒度设计。未来研究应探索捷径的根本来源，并采用更鲁棒的评估方法。

Abstract: Public datasets for agricultural disease detection are usually judged fit for use from reported metrics, which say nothing about whether the annotation scheme is internally consistent. On one public grape disease dataset (3288 images, 11995 boxes, 6 classes), varying model capacity, input resolution and detection paradigm yields a test-set mAP50 range comparable to seed-to-seed noise, with the bottleneck at small objects across all five architectures. The finding lies on the data side: one class is annotated at whole-leaf level (median box area 43.16% of the image) while the other five are annotated at lesion level. On 5156 cross-species images containing no grape, 65.7% of the false-positive boxes fall into that one class, an over-representation of 13.41x relative to its share of the training annotations. Counterfactual retraining establishes a causal effect of granularity on the magnitude of the shortcut: shrinking only that class's boxes cuts its cross-species false positives by 66%, and a placebo control confirms the effect is specific to the manipulated class. A manipulation in the opposite direction, with criteria registered in advance, returns a negative result: coarsening the finest class to whole-leaf level (0.57% to 40.37%), matched in box count and share of annotations and with higher in-distribution AP, still leaves its cross-species false positives at zero boxes, while the unmanipulated original class holds 50.0% of them. Annotation granularity is therefore a modulator of this shortcut, not its cause: it can amplify or attenuate a sink that already exists, but cannot create one, and what fixes the destination remains open. We also give a granularity screening statistic requiring neither images nor training, and show airborne lesion-level detection to be optically out of reach. The failure mode is invisible to in-distribution evaluation.

</details>


### [15] [Aristotelian Manifolds: Leveraging Platonic Perceptual Features for Backpropagation Free Rapid Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20682)
*Michael Karnes,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化了亚里士多德流形（Aristotelian Manifolds），通过分层研究揭示高容量基础模型中知识在潜在子空间中的功能合成机制。研究发现语义成熟并非单调路径，而是呈现特定几何响应特征，据此建立了可预测的层选择与特征压缩分类法，为无需反向传播地理解和利用基础模型潜在空间提供了鲁棒且可解释的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统性地表征基于柏拉图表示假说构建的广义结构框架——亚里士多德流形，并深入理解高容量基础模型内部潜在空间中知识的合成方式及几何特性。

Method: 将高容量基础模型视为通用感知滤波器，进行全面的分层调查；跨不同架构范式和多领域数据集，严格绘制网络深度、降维和距离度量之间的相互作用；通过分析潜在子空间的内部几何结构，定位流形实现峰值表征效率的确切坐标。

Result: 发现语义成熟不遵循单一的单调路径，不同数据域表现出高度不同的几何响应轮廓：专业临床模态表现为中间隆起状峰值，而自然视觉任务则表现为S型平台。基于此，建立了用于层选择和特征压缩的可预测分类法。

Conclusion: 对冻结表征的内部几何进行映射，提供了一个鲁棒的、无需反向传播且具有可解释性的框架，有助于理解和利用基础模型的潜在空间。

Abstract: This paper formalizes and systematically characterizes Aristotelian Manifolds, a generalized structural framework built upon the Platonic Representation Hypothesis. We position high-capacity foundation models as universal perceptual filters and conduct a comprehensive layer-wise investigation to map how knowledge is functionally synthesized within these latent subspaces. Across diverse architectural paradigms and multi-domain datasets, we rigorously chart the interplay between network depth, dimensionality reduction, and distance metrics. Our characterization reveals that semantic maturation does not follow a singular, monotonic path; instead, different data domains exhibit highly distinct geometric response profiles, characterized by intermediate mound-like peaks for specialized clinical modalities and sigmoidal plateaus for natural visual tasks. By profiling the exact coordinates where these manifolds achieve peak representational efficiency, we establish a predictable taxonomy for layer selection and feature compression. Ultimately, this systematic characterization demonstrates that mapping the internal geometry of frozen representations provides a robust, backpropagation-free, and interpretable framework for understanding and exploiting foundation model latent spaces.

</details>


### [16] [TopoSurfel: Closing the Loop between Gaussian Surfels and Meshes for Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20687)
*Chuanjin Fan,Wenjie Chang,Bohao Liao,Yujia Chen,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TopoSurfel框架，通过可微分等值面提取连续代理网格，利用网格引导的Surfel演化策略（法向对齐、几何感知密度控制）解决3DGS表面重建中的结构模糊和伪影问题，实现高精度几何重建与高质量视图合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的方法因缺乏显式结构化几何先验，难以直接提取高保真表面，易在无纹理或遮挡区域产生伪影和浮动点，且依赖多视图几何一致性或局部约束存在局限性。

Method: 1. 动态提取非训练可微分的连续代理网格，建立Gaussian surfels与连续网格间的闭环连接；2. 引入网格引导的Surfel演化策略，包括法向对齐和几何感知密度控制以抑制伪影并填充孔洞；3. 提出空间感知混合重初始化策略，解决大规模场景下的初始化难题。

Result: 实验表明TopoSurfel在保持高质量基于网格的新视角合成的同时，实现了具有竞争力的几何重建精度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了3DGS表面重建中的结构歧义问题，为复杂场景下的高保真几何重建提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in novel view synthesis. However, extracting high-fidelity surfaces directly from 3DGS remains challenging due to its discrete and unstructured nature. Existing 3DGS-based reconstruction methods typically rely on multi-view geometric consistency or local constraints. Without an explicit structured geometric prior during optimization, these methods often struggle to resolve structural ambiguities, leading to artifacts and floaters, particularly in textureless or occluded regions. To address this limitation, we propose TopoSurfel, a novel framework that closes the loop between Gaussian surfels and continuous meshes. Unlike recent methods that incorporate mesh extraction into the differentiable pipeline by introducing auxiliary neural networks or extra per-Gaussian parameters, we dynamically extract a continuous proxy mesh via a non-trainable differentiable iso-surfacing process. Leveraging this differentiable connection, we introduce a mesh-guided surfel evolution strategy, including normal alignment and geometry-aware density control, to effectively suppress floaters and fill surface holes. Furthermore, to address the initialization challenges in large-scale environments, we propose a spatially aware hybrid re-initialization strategy that ensures robust reconstruction across complex scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TopoSurfel achieves competitive geometric reconstruction accuracy while maintaining high-quality mesh-based novel view synthesis. The code for our method is available at https://github.com/Fan-Treasure/TopoSurfel.

</details>


### [17] [Identity-Aware Human-Object Interaction Motion Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20690)
*Yiming Wang,Yonghao Dang,Huilai Li,Jiawei Tu,Jianqin Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ID-HOINet模型，解决人类-物体交互（HOI）动作描述中缺乏主体身份识别的问题，通过多视图学习和两阶段重写策略生成包含具体身份的HOI动作描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI动作描述方法通常使用“某人”等通用术语，未将描述与具体主体身份关联，导致信息缺失。

Method: 提出ID-HOINet，包含两个核心组件：1. 多视图身份-动作学习模块（MVIML），利用BEHAVE和InterCap数据集，建模时间阶段和相机视角间的依赖关系以捕获身份和交互特征；2. 两阶段描述重写策略（TSCR），先检索身份并生成无身份信息的HOI描述，再将其重写为包含预测身份的最终描述。支持从多视图视频输入到单视图输出的推理。

Result: 实验表明ID-HOINet在该任务上达到了最先进（SOTA）的性能水平。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了身份感知的HOI动作描述生成，提升了描述的准确性和细粒度，代码将在论文接受后开源。

Abstract: Existing human-object interaction (HOI) motion captioning methods typically describe what happens while referring to the subject using generic terms such as "a person" or "someone", without grounding the caption in subject identity. To address this limitation, we introduce Identity-Aware Human-Object Interaction Motion Captioning task. This task requires each generated caption to specify both the subject identity and the corresponding HOI motion. For example, the model generates "Sub_ID lifts the chair" rather than "A person lifts the chair". For this task, we design identity-aware HOI motion captions based on the BEHAVE and InterCap datasets. We further propose ID-HOINet, which learns from multi-view videos while supporting single-view identity-aware HOI motion caption generation. ID-HOINet contains two core components: Multi-View Identity-Motion Learning Module (MVIML) and Two-Stage Caption Rewriting Strategy (TSCR). MVIML learns from multi-view videos by modeling dependencies across temporal stages and camera viewpoints, capturing identity and interaction motion features. At inference, the TSCR first retrieves the subject identity and generates identity-agnostic HOI motion captions. TSCR then rewrites these captions with the predicted identity to produce the final identity-aware HOI motion captions. Experiments demonstrate that ID-HOINet achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [18] [Bridging Language and Spherical Space: Object-Centric Control for Text-to-Panorama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20691)
*Derui Li,Qian Qiao,Yuhao Sun,Wenhao Guo,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PanoCtrl框架，通过PanoParse解析文本为球面对象级条件，并利用PanoControl在扩散模型中注入空间引导，解决现有方法在360度全景生成中方向描述与空间对齐困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到全景生成方法依赖隐式空间推理，难以忠实地将物体级别的方向性语言描述映射到球面全景场景中；而引入显式布局又降低了基于语言的交互灵活性且未根本解决自我中心方向语言与全景图像空间之间的错位问题。

Method: 提出PanoCtrl框架：1) PanoParse：文本条件解析器，预测对象语义和球面边界视野（BFoV）参数；2) PanoControl：通过对象感知注意力和空间残差增强，将对象级语义和空间指导注入扩散Transformer；3) 构建PanoGround数据集，包含对象级球面标注和多样化方向描述。

Result: 实验表明，PanoCtrl在空间对齐和图像质量方面均达到了最先进水平（SOTA）。

Conclusion: PanoCtrl通过显式桥接自然语言与球面全景空间，有效解决了可控文本到全景生成中的空间对齐难题，提升了生成的准确性和灵活性。

Abstract: Panoramic image generation is increasingly important for immersive applications such as virtual reality, augmented reality, and 3D content creation. Unlike perspective images, panoramic images represent a viewer-centered $360^\circ$ surrounding space, where directional expressions such as left, right, front, and behind play a central role in spatial understanding. However, existing text-to-panorama methods largely rely on implicit spatial reasoning and often fail to faithfully ground object-level directional descriptions in spherical panoramic scenes. A straightforward alternative is to introduce explicit layouts, but requiring manually specified spatial conditions reduces the flexibility of language-based interaction and does not directly resolve the misalignment between egocentric directional language and panoramic image space. To address this issue, we propose PanoCtrl, an object-centric framework for controllable text-to-panorama generation. Our method explicitly bridges natural language and spherical panoramic space by converting textual descriptions into structured object-level spherical conditions and integrating them into the diffusion process. Specifically, we introduce PanoParse, a text-conditioned parser that predicts object semantics and spherical bounding field-of-view (BFoV) parameters, and \textbf{PanoControl}, which injects object-level semantic and spatial guidance into the diffusion transformer through object-aware attention and spatial residual enhancement. To support this task, we construct PanoGround, a dataset with object-level spherical annotations and diverse directional descriptions for controllable panoramic generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PanoCtrl achieves state-of-the-art performance in both spatial alignment and image quality.

</details>


### [19] [ArtiMo: Agent-Driven Articulated Mesh Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20699)
*Chunyu Zou,Peng Dai,Yi-Hua Huang,Ze Yuan,Jingwei Huang,Yeming Yao,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ArtiMo框架，利用LLM/VLM代理和URDF约束，实现零样本文本引导的3D铰接网格动画生成，通过视觉自我改进机制纠正错误，并构建新基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的网格动画方法因缺乏任务特定训练数据和显式关节监督，难以满足文本动画所需的严格运动学约束、部件间因果交互建模及指令忠实度要求。

Method: 提出ArtiMo代理驱动框架：1) 结合LLM/VLM进行运动生成编排；2) 融合URDF显式运动学约束与代理推理规划能力，无需微调即可生成因果连贯的运动；3) 引入视觉自我改进机制，将动画渲染为关键帧和运动线索，由VLM迭代诊断并修正错误。

Result: 构建了涵盖21类铰接对象的新基准数据集（含高质量因果运动标注）；实验表明ArtiMo显著优于基线方法，尤其在复杂因果驱动运动中表现突出。

Conclusion: ArtiMo通过代理推理、显式约束和视觉反馈闭环，有效解决了文本引导铰接网格动画中的运动学和因果性问题，为无监督/零样本场景下的3D动画生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Animating articulated 3D meshes via text requires satisfying strict kinematic constraints, modeling causal interactions between parts, and achieving instruction fidelity. Due to the absence of task-specific training data and explicit articulation supervision, existing data-driven mesh animation methods are largely inapplicable to this setting. To address this, we propose ArtiMo, a novel agent-driven framework for text-guided articulated mesh animation. Operating in a zero-shot manner, ArtiMo develops an agentic pipeline powered by Large Language and Vision-Language Models (LLMs/VLMs) to orchestrate motion generation. By synergizing the explicit kinematic constraints of URDF with the agent's reasoning and planning capabilities, it effectively produces causally coherent part motions and interactions without requiring model fine-tuning. To ensure motion correctness, the agent additionally utilizes a visual self-improvement mechanism: generated animations are rendered into compact keyframes and motion cues, enabling the VLM to iteratively diagnose and correct errors. Furthermore, we contribute a new benchmark dataset spanning 21 articulated object categories, featuring high-quality motion annotations enriched with causal relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ArtiMo significantly outperforms baselines, particularly on complex, causally driven motions. The project page is available at https://zou-2004.github.io/ArtiMo/.

</details>


### [20] [AGIDefect-4K: A Richly Annotated Dataset for AI-Generated Image Defect Detection, Localization and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20713)
*Xiangfei Sheng,Weidong Zou,Tianjiao Gu,Zhichao Yang,Pengfei Chen,Leida Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AGIDefect-4K数据集和AGIDA框架，解决AI生成图像缺陷诊断不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI能产生逼真图像，但存在关键缺陷影响可靠性；现有评估基准在全面缺陷诊断方面探索不足。

Method: 构建包含4,000张图像、15种生成模型的AGIDefect-4K数据集，提供层级化标注（检测标签、像素级分割掩码、文本解释及质量评分）；基于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）提出AGIDA基线框架，联合执行缺陷检测、定位、解释和质量预测。

Result: 在AGIDefect-4K上的综合基准测试表明，AI生成图像的缺陷理解仍具挑战性，验证了该数据集的价值。

Conclusion: AGIDefect-4K填补了全面AI生成图像缺陷诊断的空白，为后续研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Generative AI can now produce highly realistic images, yet current models still exhibit subtle but critical defects that undermine their reliability. While existing AI-generated image (AGI) evaluation benchmarks have made notable progress, comprehensive AGI defect diagnosis remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce AGIDefect-4K, a richly annotated dataset of 4,000 images from 15 state-of-the-art generative models spanning both open-source and closed-source systems. AGIDefect-4K features hierarchical defect annotations: (1) detection labels identifying whether defects exist, (2) pixel-level segmentation masks localizing defective regions, and (3) detailed textual explanations characterizing defect types and their perceptual impact. Each image is further annotated with an overall quality score. Building on this, we present AGIDA (AGI Defect Assistant), a baseline framework leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for joint defect detection, localization, explanation, and quality prediction. Comprehensive benchmarking on AGIDefect-4K reveals that AGI defect understanding remains challenging, underscoring the value of this dataset. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/sxfly99/AGIDefect-4K.

</details>


### [21] [AffordAny: Open-World 3D Affordance Grounding from Monocular RGB Images via Vision-Language-Guided Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20720)
*Junqi Wu,Kaihua Tang,Xuanwen Chen,Hongzhi Li,Jianqiang Huang,Xian-Sheng Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AffordAny框架，利用单目RGB图像和冻结的VLM解码器实现开放世界3D功能部件定位，通过自动生成的基准数据和伪标签自训练显著提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预构建的物体中心3D几何和封闭的功能本体论，限制了从原始RGB观测中进行部署的能力。

Method: 1. 使用自动化流水线从单目RGB图像构建大规模文本条件3D部件监督数据；2. 采用冻结的Vision-Language Model (VLM) 引导解码器，通过空间投影、指令条件语义压缩和双向几何-语义交互融合特征；3. 利用最小扰动伪标签自训练提升开放世界泛化性。

Result: 构建了包含5,334个物体和10,633个部件级样本（涵盖473个类别）的基准，类别多样性比先前工作高一个数量级。在未见物体上IoU为0.428，未见类别上IoU为0.315，未见类别mIoU相对提升6.3%（p<0.01），指令敏感性差距仅为0.105。

Conclusion: 该方法在开放世界场景下展现了有效性和鲁棒性，能够无需人工标注地扩展新物体并适应未见过的类别和指令变体。

Abstract: Open-world 3D affordance grounding requires localizing functional object parts in 3D given free-form language queries. Existing methods typically assume pre-built object-centric 3D geometry and closed affordance ontologies, limiting deployment from raw RGB observations. We present AffordAny, an end-to-end framework that uses one monocular RGB image to construct large-scale text-conditioned 3D part supervision, ground affordances with a frozen vision-language model (VLM) guided decoder, and improve open-world generalization through pseudo-label self-training. Our automated pipeline produces a benchmark of 5,334 objects and 10,633 part-level samples spanning 473 categories, an order-of-magnitude increase in categorical diversity over prior work. The decoder progressively fuses frozen Cosmos-2B features with 3D geometry through spatial projection, instruction-conditioned semantic compression, and bidirectional geometry-semantics interaction. Minimal-perturbation pseudo-label self-training further adds new objects without human annotation. Under a systematic generalization protocol evaluating unseen objects, unseen categories, and unseen instruction paraphrases, our approach achieves 0.428 IoU on unseen objects and 0.315 IoU on unseen categories after self-training, with unseen-category mIoU improving by 6.3% relative (p<0.01) and an instruction sensitivity gap of only 0.105, demonstrating effectiveness and robustness of our method.

</details>


### [22] [VisTa3D: A Dataset and Benchmark for Thin Object Reconstruction from Vision, Tactile, and 3D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20740)
*Shania Guo,Yeongsik Seo,Andrew Fu,Mei Hao,Iris Xia,Jiwon Jenny Lee,Xinyi Mary Xie,Hyoungseob Park,Aaron Dollar,Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有3D重建模型在细薄物体上表现不佳的问题，本文发布了首个包含同步RGB、深度和触觉数据的VisTa3D数据集，并提出了一种视觉-范围-触觉融合的3D重建基线模型，验证了触觉数据对提升细薄物体重建保真度的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D重建模型（无论是基于视觉、深度还是两者结合）在处理细薄物体时往往性能不足，主要原因是这类物体在RGB图像和3D点云中占据的空间较小，导致特征信息稀疏。

Method: 1. 构建VisTa3D数据集：包含387个场景、70种细薄物体和17种环境，提供同步的RGB图像、深度图、触觉响应图，以及惯性测量、相机姿态/标定和激光扫描得到的地面真实深度及分割图。2. 基准测试：在当前3D重建模型上评估其在VisTa3D上的表现。3. 提出新模型：引入首个视觉-范围-触觉（Visual-Range-Tactile）3D重建模型作为基线，利用触觉数据提供的局部形状和变形信息进行辅助重建。

Result: 基准测试证实了现有3D重建模型在细薄物体上确实存在低保真度问题。通过引入触觉数据，提出的新模型旨在改善这一缺陷，证明了触觉反馈在细薄物体重建中的潜在价值。

Conclusion: 触觉数据能够为细薄物体提供关键的局部形状和变形信息，有助于解决现有模型因空间占比小而导致的重建精度低的问题。VisTa3D数据集和新模型的发布为后续研究提供了重要的基准和数据支持。

Abstract: State-of-the-art 3D reconstruction models, whether from visual, range, or both, tend to underperform on thin objects. This is partially due to the small amount of space such objects occupy in RGB images and in 3D point clouds. To test the extent of their errors, we collected the first thin object dataset comprising of synchronized RGB images, depth maps, and tactile response maps, where each frame is associated with inertial measurements, camera pose and calibration, and groundtruth depth and segmentation maps obtained from laser scanning of thin objects. We hypothesize that tactile data can aid in the reconstruction of thin objects as their response maps provide local shape and deformation information. Our dataset, termed VisTa3D, comprises of 387 scenes covering 70 thin objects over 17 environments. We benchmarked current 3D reconstruction models on VisTa3D and found that, indeed, they exhibit low fidelity on thin objects. To test if tactile data can help, we introduce the first visual-range-tactile 3D reconstruction model as a baseline. Code and data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/shaniaguo/VisTa3D.

</details>


### [23] [Generating Multi-view Adversarial Examples for Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20748)
*Qi Song,Ziyuan Luo,Haoliang Han,Renjie Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVAP-G，一种针对3D重建模型VGGT的多视图对抗攻击生成器，通过单次前向传播产生跨视图一致且不可察觉的扰动，无需迭代优化即可显著降低模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗扰动需昂贵的逐场景优化，而通用对抗扰动（UAPs）依赖静态模式，无法有效攻击高性能3D基础模型VGGT，存在安全漏洞风险。

Method: 设计MVAP-G多视图对抗扰动生成器，引入跨视图对抗对齐机制处理多视图图像，在单次前向传递中生成跨视图一致的不可察觉扰动。

Result: 实验表明MVAP-G在推理阶段无需迭代优化即可显著降级VGGT性能，揭示了3D基础模型的严重脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该工作开创了针对3D基础模型的多视图对抗攻击，强调了构建鲁棒3D视觉系统的紧迫性。

Abstract: The Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) enables unified feed-forward 3D reconstruction from multi-view images. However, deploying such a high-performance model may expose critical security vulnerabilities. Traditional adversarial perturbations require costly per-scene optimization, while Universal Adversarial Perturbations (UAPs) rely on a single static pattern and fail to effectively attack VGGT. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MVAP-G}, a multi-view adversarial perturbation generator that produces imperceptible consistent perturbations across multiple views in a single feed-forward pass. To ensure perturbation consistency across diverse scenes, we design a cross-view adversarial alignment mechanism to process multi-view images. Experiments demonstrate that MVAP-G significantly degrades VGGT performance without iterative optimization during inference. This work pioneers multi-view adversarial attacks on 3D foundation models, uncovering severe vulnerabilities and underscoring the urgent need for robust 3D vision systems. The code is available at https://github.com/qsong2001/mvap-g.

</details>


### [24] [Identity-Preserving Text-to-Video Generation via Agentic Enhancement and Semantic Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20749)
*Jiayi Gao,Changcheng Hua,Jiaqi Tang,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AESR框架，通过全局代理提示增强和样本级视觉语义修复模块，解决闭源视频生成模型的身份漂移和指令遵循问题，在ACM MM 2026挑战赛中获Track 1第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业视频生成模型虽视觉质量好，但存在身份漂移、指令遵循不完整及复杂提示下细节缺失等问题，且因模型封闭无法直接优化参数。

Method: 提出Agentic Enhancement and Semantic Repair (AESR)框架：1. 全局代理提示增强模块：学习官方提示格式，从人机交互数据获取先验，并通过代理循环积累经验形成可复用剧本；2. 样本级视觉语义修复模块：利用VLM定位错误片段并设计修复指令，将帧编辑为显式视觉参考以指导视频编辑模型修复局部错误；3. 轻量级混合专家选择策略以筛选可靠输出。

Result: 在ACM MM 2026身份保持视频生成挑战赛的官方评估协议下，系统MIPL_Video在Track 1中排名第一，证明了AESR在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: AESR是一种轻量级增强框架，能有效提升闭源模型的身份保持视频生成质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation aims to synthesize videos that follow natural-language instructions while maintaining the visual identity of a given subject. Recent commercial video generation models have achieved strong visual quality and motion realism, but they still suffer from identity drift, incomplete instruction following, and missing visual details under complex prompts. Since these models are usually closed-source black boxes, directly improving them through parameter optimization is often infeasible. We therefore propose Agentic Enhancement and Semantic Repair (AESR), a lightweight enhancement framework for identity-preserving video generation. To improve prompt construction before generation and mitigate the above failures, AESR introduces a global agentic prompt enhancement module. This module learns model-specific prompting formats from official documentation, acquires human-centered video generation priors from human-interaction data, and accumulates test-domain identity-preserving generation experience into a reusable playbook through an agentic loop. To further repair errors in videos generated with enhanced prompts, AESR introduces a sample-level visual semantic repair module, which uses a VLM to locate erroneous video segments and design repair instructions, edits selected frames into explicit visual references, and guides a video editing model to fix local semantic or identity-related errors. We also adopt a lightweight Mixture-of-Experts selection strategy to choose reliable outputs from different generation and refinement paths. Under the official evaluation protocol of the ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge, our system MIPL\_Video ranked first in Track 1, demonstrating the effectiveness of AESR for practical identity-preserving video generation. The code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/AESR.

</details>


### [25] [SPARK-SAM: Self-Prompt Adaptation with Response Knowledge for SAM in Infrared Small Target Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20754)
*Aji Mao,Zhenming Peng,Bailin Mu,Tian Pu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPARK-SAM模型，通过自提示适应与响应知识解决SAM在红外小目标分割中的空间提示与掩码响应不匹配问题，显著提升IoU指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接迁移可提示分割模型（如SAM）到自动红外小目标分割（IRSTD）时，存在空间提示与目标域掩码响应之间的不匹配，导致性能低下（官方SAM2.1 IoU仅约2-4%）。

Method: 引入SPARK-SAM（Self-Prompt Adaptation with Response Knowledge for SAM），学习目标域响应知识，并通过图像条件的联合自提示状态对解码器进行条件约束。训练结合基准掩码监督与可靠性感知的响应引导。

Result: 在NUAA-SIRST、NUDT-SIRST和IRSTD-1K数据集上分别达到75.78%、86.49%和68.34%的IoU，仅增加0.726M参数，在14种重新训练的SAM变体及适配方法中排名第一。

Conclusion: 响应适应使预测点获得可靠目标定位前即可达成大部分最终IoU；提示监督使预测提示候选与目标位置对齐；消融实验验证了响应引导和高分辨率提示 refinement 带来的持续精度提升。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation models provide a reusable interface, but direct transfer to automatic infrared small-target segmentation (IRSTD) exposes a mismatch between spatial prompts and target-domain mask responses. In a diagnostic using target-covering loose-box prompts deterministically derived from test reference masks, the best official SAM2.1 results are only 4.69%, 1.64%, and 2.28% IoU on NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, and IRSTD-1K. We introduce SPARK-SAM (Self-Prompt Adaptation with Response Knowledge for SAM), which learns target-domain response knowledge and conditions the decoder through an image-conditioned joint self-prompt state. Training combines benchmark-mask supervision with reliability-aware response guidance. SPARK-SAM achieves 75.78%, 86.49%, and 68.34% IoU with 0.726M additional parameters, ranking first on two benchmarks among 14 retrained SAM variants and adaptations evaluated as automatic image-to-mask methods. The staged IRSTD-1K diagnostic shows that response adaptation reaches most of the final IoU before the predicted points acquire reliable target grounding. Prompt supervision aligns the predicted prompt candidates with target locations, and frozen-weight interventions measure output sensitivity to the joint self-prompt state. Matched ablations show consistent accuracy gains from response guidance and high-resolution prompt refinement across all three datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/Sakauma/SPARK-SAM.

</details>


### [26] [Vis-Poison: Poisoning Visual Knowledge in Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20756)
*Rujin Liang,Zhongpu Chen,Yuhao Lei,Xin Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vis-Poison攻击，通过构造视觉上可信的中毒图像直接污染多模态RAG系统的知识库，无需修改文本元数据。该方法在黑盒设置下对3万条目知识库的攻击成功率高达40.16%-65.40%，且对仅依赖参数化知识即可正确回答的多模态大模型仍保持超过60%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对多模态检索增强生成（RAG）系统的攻击主要依赖于篡改文本元数据（如标题、摘要等），但实际应用中图像本身作为外部知识来源的重要性日益增加。为了评估直接污染视觉证据所带来的安全风险，需要研究一种不依赖文本操纵、直接利用图像作为攻击载荷的新型攻击方式。

Method: 提出Vis-Poison攻击框架，采用自动化的多智能体（multi-agent）方法构建视觉上合理且包含恶意内容的中毒图像。这些图像被注入到多模态RAG管道中，作为检索到的外部知识源。实验评估涵盖两种代表性的多模态RAG流水线、四种嵌入模型和六种生成模型。

Result: 1. 在黑盒设置下，针对3万条目的多模态知识库，Vis-Poison实现了40.16%至65.40%的端到端攻击成功率。2. 即使面对能够仅凭参数化知识正确回答问题的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），Vis-Poison依然有效，平均成功率超过60%。3. 证明了仅通过操控图像内容而非文本元数据，也能严重损害多模态生成系统的安全性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 视觉知识 poisoning 是一种严重威胁多模态 RAG 系统安全的新兴攻击向量。由于攻击者可以直接控制图像内容而无需触碰关联文本，这种攻击具有隐蔽性强、难以防御的特点。研究结果强调了在构建多模态 RAG 系统时，必须加强对视觉输入内容的验证与清洗机制，不能仅依赖文本元数据的完整性检查。

Abstract: While multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems increasingly rely on images as external knowledge sources, the introduction of poisoned visual evidence can severely compromise multimodal large language model (MLLM) generation. Unlike prior attacks that rely on altering textual metadata, we introduce Vis-Poison, a novel visual knowledge poisoning attack where the poisoned image itself is the attacker-controlled payload, without manipulating captions, summaries, metadata, or other associated text. Specifically, this attack is instantiated through an automated multi-agent method that constructs visually plausible poisoned images. To assess its impact, we evaluate Vis-Poison across two representative multimodal RAG pipelines, four embedding models, and six generation models. Empirically, Vis-Poison achieves an end-to-end attack success rate of 40.16\% to 65.40\% against 30k-entry multimodal knowledge bases in \emph{black-box} settings. Moreover, Vis-Poison remains effective against various MLLMs that can answer correctly from parametric knowledge alone, with an average success rate above 60\%. Code and data are available at https://github.com/SWUFE-DB-Group/Vis-Poison.

</details>


### [27] [DiGS-Avatar: Single-Image Animatable 3D Human Reconstruction via UV-Space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20759)
*Jiakun Li,Li Fang,Hao Zhu,Fei Hu,Long Ye,Yuan Zhang,Jinyao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiGS-Avatar，一种基于扩散模型的单图3D人体重建方法。通过教师-学生框架生成几何对齐的伪真值潜变量，结合语义特征恢复纹理细节，最终解码为3D高斯图元，实现0.71秒快速重建且保持3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单图像3D人体重建中纹理过度平滑和几何不一致的问题；同时克服现有扩散模型依赖多视图合成导致计算昂贵及视图不一致的缺陷。

Method: 将任务重构为基于扩散的UV潜变量补全任务。引入教师-学生框架：多视图教师网络提供几何对齐的伪真值潜变量以监督单视图扩散学生网络。利用推断出的潜变量作为结构骨架，注入高级语义特征以恢复精细纹理细节，最后解码为3D高斯图元。

Result: 实验表明，DiGS-Avatar在视觉保真度和零样本泛化能力上达到最先进或极具竞争力的水平，并能仅用0.71秒重建完全可动画的3D化身。

Conclusion: 该方法通过设计确保3D一致性的扩散式UV潜变量补全机制，有效平衡了生成质量、计算效率与几何准确性，实现了高效的高质量3D人体重建。

Abstract: Single-image 3D human reconstruction often suffers from over-smoothed textures and geometric inconsistencies. While diffusion models improve generative quality, their reliance on multi-view synthesis prior to 3D reconstruction is computationally expensive and prone to view inconsistency. We propose DiGS-Avatar, which reformulates this task as an efficient, diffusion-based UV-latent completion task, ensuring 3D consistency by design. To capture accurate spatial structure, we introduce a teacher-student framework where a multi-view teacher provides geometrically aligned pseudo-ground-truth latents to supervise a single-view diffusion student. Treating this inferred latent as a robust structural skeleton, our method injects high-level semantic features to accurately recover fine textural details without disrupting spatial integrity. The refined representation is then decoded into 3D Gaussian primitives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiGS-Avatar achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive visual fidelity and zero-shot generalization, while reconstructing a fully animatable 3D avatar in just 0.71 seconds. Code is available at https://github.com/KLMAV-CUC/DiGS-Avatar.

</details>


### [28] [CARD: Diagnosing Belief to Action Routing Failures in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20763)
*Souptik Kumar Majumdar,Fabian Kögel,Andreas Bulling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CARD方法诊断VLM跨轴路由失败，发现模型虽内部表征信念等心理状态，但无法将其用于指导后续动作预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知视觉语言模型（VLMs）内部表征了代理的信念、知识和意图等心理状态，但不清楚这些表征是否以及如何被下游预测所利用，旨在填补这一认知空白。

Method: 引入跨轴路由诊断（CARD）方法，通过沿一个轴引导激活并测量另一个轴预测的响应来进行诊断；在提出的合作网格世界基准Relay Chain上对开放权重VLMs进行测试。

Result: 诊断出关键的路由失败：模型未能将信念表征纳入其下一步动作预测中，导致关于合作伙伴的宝贵信息未被有效利用。

Conclusion: VLMs内部的心理状态表征与其实际决策行为之间存在脱节，模型未能有效整合内部信念以优化合作预测。

Abstract: Linear probes and activation steering have uncovered that vision-language models (VLMs) internally represent mental states such as agents' beliefs, knowledge, and intentions. However, it is unclear whether and how these representations are used by downstream predictions along these axes. To close this gap, we introduce Cross-Axis Routing Diagnostic (CARD), which steers activations along one axis while measuring the response of a different axis's prediction. Applied to open-weight VLMs on Relay Chain -- a new cooperative grid-world benchmark we propose -- we diagnose a critical routing failure: models fail to incorporate belief representations into their next action prediction, effectively leaving valuable information about their partners unused.

</details>


### [29] [MotionPhys: Detecting AI-Generated Videos via Physical Consistency of Optical-Flow Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20770)
*Haojin He,Hao Tan,Zichang Tan,Ajian Liu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MotionPhys框架，通过稀疏运动轨迹的几何演化检测AI生成视频中的物理不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI视频生成模型虽具有高视觉保真度，但缺乏对现实世界物理约束（如惯性、连续力）的显式遵循，导致在长序列动作中物理运动一致性缺失。

Method: 构建轻量级可解释框架MotionPhys，将稀疏运动轨迹视为物理证据而非外观伪影；通过建模多时间尺度下的轨迹几何演化，捕捉细微的运动不一致性并转化为紧凑表示以进行检测。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，MotionPhys能有效检测生成视频中的物理不一致性，并在不同视频生成器之间具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于物理轨迹几何分析的方法能比传统视觉线索更有效地识别AI生成视频中的物理缺陷，为评估生成视频的物理真实性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Modern AI video generation models can produce videos with high visual fidelity and seemingly smooth temporal transitions. However, visual realism does not necessarily imply physical motion consistency. Existing generative models mainly optimize distribution matching in pixel or latent spaces, without explicitly enforcing real-world constraints such as inertia, continuous forces, and trajectory geometry. Our experiments show that AI-generated videos remain visually plausible over short sequences of consecutive frames, yet fail to preserve physical motion consistency throughout a complete object action, resulting in systematic statistical discrepancies in their motion trajectories. Based on this observation, we introduce MotionPhys, a lightweight and interpretable framework that treats sparse motion trajectories as physical evidence rather than relying on appearance artifacts or generator-specific traces. By modeling the geometric evolution of trajectories across multiple temporal scales, MotionPhys reveals subtle motion inconsistencies that are difficult to capture with conventional visual cues and transforms them into a compact representation for efficient detection. Experiments on multiple datasets show that MotionPhys can effectively detect physical inconsistencies in generated videos and generalizes well across different video generators.

</details>


### [30] [M2Depth: Unifying Monocular Depth Foundation Priors with Multi-View Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20788)
*Byeonggwon Lee,Sanggi Lee,Siwoo Lee,Khang Truong Giang,Soohwan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合深度基础模型（DFM）与级联多视图立体（MVS）框架的新方法，通过双向互 refinement 和先验引导的成本体优化，解决传统单目深度先验融合不充分的问题，显著提升深度估计的完整性、泛化能力及精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的 MVS 在未见场景（特别是遮挡区域或视重叠有限区域）泛化能力差；虽引入单目深度基础模型（DFM）提供先验，但现有方法采用静态单向融合，未能充分利用多视图与单目模态的互补优势。

Method: 1. 构建 DFM 与级联 MVS 管道的紧密耦合框架，实施双向相互精炼策略：利用 MVS 深度解决单目预测的尺度模糊问题，同时利用单目深度增强 MVS 估计的结构完整性和细粒度细节。2. 引入先验引导的成本体精炼机制，通过基于注意力的融合和离散化深度积分箱，有效整合多视图与单目信息，促进局部几何一致性。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，该方法优于最先进（SOTA）的 MVS 方法，生成更完整、泛化性更强且边界清晰的深度图。尽管未专门针对稀疏视角设计，其仍表现出卓越的泛化能力，能与专用稀疏视图方法竞争，并保持更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 通过双向互精炼和注意力融合机制，有效结合了 MVS 的多视图几何约束与 DFMs 的单目语义/结构先验，显著提升了深度估计性能，尤其在复杂场景和稀疏视角下具有鲁棒性和高效性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based Multi-View Stereo (MVS) has advanced significantly but often generalizes poorly to unseen scenes, particularly in occluded areas or regions with limited view overlap. To mitigate this, recent approaches integrate Depth Foundation Models (DFMs) into MVS pipelines to provide monocular depth priors. However, existing methods typically rely on a static, one-way fusion scheme, which fails to fully exploit the complementary strengths of both modalities. We propose a novel framework that overcomes this limitation by tightly coupling a DFM with a cascade MVS pipeline through a bidirectional mutual refinement strategy. Our method leverages MVS depth to resolve the scale ambiguity in monocular predictions, while the monocular depth, in turn, enhances the structural completeness and fine-grained detail of the MVS estimate. Furthermore, we introduce a prior-guided cost volume refinement mechanism that effectively integrates multi-view and monocular information via attention-based fusion and discretized depth bins, thereby promoting local geometric consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art MVS approaches on standard benchmarks, producing more complete and generalizable depth maps with sharp boundaries. Furthermore, although not explicitly designed for sparse-view settings, our framework generalizes remarkably well, competing favorably with even dedicated sparse-view methods while maintaining a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [31] [CertVLA: Certified Defense against Physical Visual Attacks for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20791)
*Hui Lu,Zhijie Peng,Yuqi Lin,Zaijia Yang,Jiaming He,Shuhan Ye,Yi Yu,Hanwei Zhu,Bingquan Shen,Alex Kot,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CertVLA，一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的认证防御机制，能有效抵御有界的补丁和纹理攻击，确保闭环控制中动作的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的认证补丁防御主要针对离散标签，无法直接为连续且时间相关的动作提供认证；同时VLA策略易受局部物理扰动影响。

Method: 通过校准行为一致动作区域和使用确定性覆盖掩码（deterministic covering masks），标准化动作分歧并验证一致性；利用最大-最小-最大（max-min-max）剧集得分进行有限样本清洁覆盖率校准，并将查询级决策扩展至完整闭环 rollout。

Result: 证明了在满足有界支持威胁模型的自适应攻击下，CertVLA认证的每次 rollout 仅执行与擦除攻击后的清洁预测一致的动作块；在双掩码 rollout 正确性条件下，进一步保证任务成功。实验在仿真和真实世界中验证了其对抗补丁攻击的有效性，并在仿真中验证了对抗纹理攻击的效果。

Conclusion: CertVLA提供了一种与补丁内容、生成方法及物理变换无关的认证机制，有效解决了VLA策略在连续动作空间下的安全防御问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are vulnerable to localized physical perturbations, yet existing certified patch defenses target discrete labels and cannot directly certify continuous, temporally correlated actions. We introduce CertVLA, a certified defense for closed-loop VLA control under bounded patch and texture attacks. CertVLA proposes a calibrated region of behaviorally consistent actions, while deterministic covering masks ensure that at least one checked prediction is attack-free. Specifically, CertVLA normalizes action disagreement by the benign variation of each mask pair and accepts a single-mask anchor only when it remains consistent under every second mask. It then calibrates the resulting max-min-max episode score to provide finite-sample clean coverage. Conjoining query-level decisions extends the action certificate to the complete closed-loop rollout. Furthermore, we prove that against any adaptive attacker satisfying the bounded-support threat model, every rollout certified by CertVLA executes only action chunks consistent with attack-erased clean predictions. Under dual-mask rollout correctness, this consistency certificate further guarantees task success. The certificate is independent of patch content, generation method, and physical transformation. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate the empirical and certified effectiveness of CertVLA against patch attacks, with additional simulation validation on texture attacks.

</details>


### [32] [Routing Before Looking: Query-Adaptive Evidence Acquisition for Long-form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20805)
*Tianyue Wang,Xuying Wu,Yuxiang Ma,Ruiming Liang,Jiaxuan Kang,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Leigang Qu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Route2Look框架，通过动态路由策略自适应选择证据获取工具（全局浏览、时间定位、语义检索），解决长视频理解中查询与证据获取不匹配的问题，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有“先规划后感知”方法通常依赖单一的主导策略（生成式或检索式），难以灵活应对多样化的查询需求，导致长视频理解效果受限。

Method: 构建轻量级、模型无关的Route2Look框架，采用“路由-查看-记忆”循环机制。核心是通过差分对比分析从生成式和检索式轨迹中蒸馏路由技能，并结合硬路由规则及继续/停止标准进行推理时的动态工具选择。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的长视频基准测试中，Route2Look实现了最先进的性能，同时在各种数据集和查询类型下保持了强大的帧效率。Oracle路由分析进一步验证了查询自适应证据获取的潜力。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的证据获取策略对于提升长视频理解能力具有重要价值，Route2Look为这一方向提供了高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding remains challenging for video agents due to the mismatch between query demands and evidence acquisition strategies. Although recent planning-before-perception methods outperform query-agnostic pipelines, they often rely on a single dominant strategy, either generation-based strategy or retrieval-based strategy, limiting their ability to handle diverse query demands. We propose Route2Look, a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for query-adaptive evidence acquisition in long-form video understanding. Route2Look operates in a Route-Look-Memorize loop with three tools: Global Browse for holistic context, Temporal Ground for explicit temporal cues, and Semantic Retrieve for semantic search. The core component is a routing policy that dynamically selects evidence acquisition tools based on the query. To build this policy, Route2Look adopts a two-stage design: first distilling the routing skill from differential contrastive analysis between generation-based and retrieval-based trajectories, and then applying the distilled skill with hard routing rules and continue-or-stop criteria during inference. Experiments on challenging long-video benchmarks show that Route2Look achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining strong frame efficiency across datasets and query types. Oracle routing analysis further reveals the potential of query-adaptive evidence acquisition for future long-form video understanding.

</details>


### [33] [TRACE: Training-time Report-guided and Clinically Ordered Concept Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20809)
*Wentao Yue,Tianyou Lai,Jiayu Luo,Qingyu Mao,Ziying Wang,Zhenyuan Ning,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，利用训练时的结构化报告作为概念监督，实现测试时仅凭图像进行可解释且鲁棒的乳腺超声诊断，并解决标注不全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端深度学习缺乏可解释性和鲁棒性；现有的基于概念的方法通常假设标注完整或推理时需多模态输入，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出TRACE框架：1) 利用结构化放射学报告作为特权概念监督；2) 在恶性感知有序概念空间内通过教师引导的编辑机制细化图像衍生概念；3) 引入战略概念缺失训练(SCMT)和图像-only自编辑器以处理不完整标注。同时构建BUSC基准数据集。

Result: 在多个数据集上，TRACE相比现有方法实现了更优越的性能和更强的跨域鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TRACE通过结合训练时报告和测试时单模态输入，有效提升了乳腺超声诊断的可解释性、鲁棒性及实际适用性。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound diagnosis relies on clinically meaningful semantic concepts, yet most deep learning methods adopt end-to-end image-to-label paradigms that lack interpretability and robustness. While concept-based approaches offer a promising alternative, they often assume complete annotations or require multimodal inputs at inference, which significantly limits their real-world applicability. To tackle these issues, we propose Training-time Report-guided and Clinically Ordered Concept Editing (TRACE), a training-time report-guided framework that leverages structured radiology reports as privileged concept supervision while enabling image-only diagnosis at test time. TRACE refines image-derived concepts through a teacher-guided editing mechanism within a malignancy-aware ordered concept space. To address incomplete annotations, we introduce Strategic Concept Missing Training (SCMT) and train an image-only self-editor via edit distillation for autonomous concept refinement. Besides, we introduce BUSC, a concept-enriched benchmark linking images, labels, and structured attributes. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that TRACE achieves superior performance and improved cross-domain robustness compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [34] [Enhancing Localized Reasoning for Long Video Understanding via Efficient Segment-to-Video Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20814)
*Beibei Zhang,Chao Xu,Jun Lan,Zongyi Li,Lai Wei,Huijia Zhu,Tongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S2V方法，通过基于视频片段生成问答对并反向监督全视频训练，解决长视频理解中的噪声干扰和推理效率问题。该方法仅需简单奖励的强化学习和少量数据，实现高精度与高效率的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视频理解方面表现优异，但长视频理解（LVU）仍面临挑战：复杂且冗长的上下文中的干扰噪声会掩盖局部细节，导致模型产生错误答案。现有缓解方法主要存在两个问题：一是依赖的深度推理强化微调框架（RFT）带来高昂的训练开销（如标注成本和复杂的奖励设计）；二是自我反思和迭代感知机制导致输出冗长、推理延迟高。

Method: 提出“片段到视频监督”（Segment-to-Video Supervision, S2V）方法。具体步骤包括：1. 基于视频局部片段生成问答对（VQA），利用短片段天然关注细节的特性捕捉易被忽略的信息；2. 将这些基于片段的VQA数据迁移至整个视频进行训练，强制模型将细粒度细节与问答正确关联，同时避免全视频视角下的噪声干扰；3. 训练过程仅使用基于准确率的简单奖励强化学习（RL），且仅需10K个VQA样本；4. 推理阶段采用单次前向传播，限制输出token数量。

Result: 实验结果表明，S2V在多个LVU基准测试中持续提升了性能。相比通用MLLMs和基于推理的方法，S2V不仅在长视频理解准确率上更优，而且在训练效率和推理效率方面也显著领先。

Conclusion: S2V通过创新的片段级监督策略，有效解决了长视频理解中的细节丢失和噪声干扰问题，同时克服了现有方法在高计算成本和低推理效率方面的缺陷，为高效、高精度的长视频理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Though Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown impressive potential in video understanding, long video understanding (LVU) remains challenging since distracting noise in complex and lengthy contexts can obscure localized details, misleading MLLMs to produce incorrect answers. Recent works mitigate these issues by incentivizing deep reasoning to include relevant evidence. However, these methods have two main problems: First, the reinforcement fine-tuning framework (RFT) they leveraged incurs substantial training overheads, including high annotation costs and complicated reward designs. Second, the self-reflective and iterative-perception mechanism in some methods causes lengthy outputs and high inference latency. To alleviate these problems, we propose a novel Segment-to-Video Supervision} method (S2V) to efficiently enhance fine-grained reasoning in LVU. Specifically, we generate question answer pairs (VQA) based on localized segments, and then transfer these segment-based VQA back to the whole video for training. Due to focusing on short segments, segment-based VQA can naturally notice details which tend to be overlooked from a whole-video perspective. Training on such data can enforce MLLMs to correctly associate fine-grained details with QA while avoiding distracting noise in the whole video. The S2V training involves just reinforcement learning (RL) with a simple accuracy reward based on only 10K VQA samples and the resulting S2V model predicts answer using a single forward pass with limited output tokens. Experimental results demonstrate that S2V can consistently improve LVU performance across multiple LVU benchmarks, outperforming both general MLLMs and reasoning-based methods not only in LVU accuracy but also in training and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [35] [Identify, Locate, Link: End-to-End Key-Value Extraction from Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20868)
*A. Said Gurbuz,Ahmed Nassar,Christoph Auer,Maksym Lysak,Lucas Morin,Matteo Omenetti,Tim Strohmeyer,Panagiotis Vagenas,Nikolaos Livathinos,Michele Dolfi,Peter Staar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并微调了轻量级端到端文档解析模型SmolDocling，通过扩展DocTags标签体系和数据增强策略，在保持极小参数量（256M）的同时，实现了优于大型零样本VLM的键值对提取性能，且推理速度提升5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档处理流程依赖OCR引擎与下游模型的级联，导致多阶段误差传播；同时现有方法在处理复杂文档结构时往往难以兼顾效率与精度，缺乏高效的端到端解决方案。

Method: 1. 基于256M参数的视觉语言模型（VLM）SmolDocling进行微调，实现从图像到结构化信息的端到端提取，无需OCR预处理。
2. 扩展DocTags格式，引入专门的键、值、区域和链接标签，支持统一输出序列中的多对多关系。
3. 设计结合合成表单填充和基于图裁剪的数据增强流水线，以保留完整的键值子图结构。
4. 引入布局感知评估框架，结合文本匹配与空间边界框验证。

Result: 在FUNSD、XFUND及大规模私有数据集上，该模型在布局感知评估下表现优于更大的零样本VLM基线；其参数量仅为Qwen2.5-VL (7B)的1/27，推理速度快5倍以上。

Conclusion: SmolDocling证明了紧凑型VLM可通过改进标签体系和数据增强，高效解决文档中的识别、定位和关联问题，为低成本、高效率的文档信息提取提供了新范式。

Abstract: Document processing pipelines traditionally cascade optical character recognition (OCR) engines with downstream models for structured information extraction, leading to multi-stage error propagation. We fine-tune SmolDocling, a compact 256M-parameter vision-language model (VLM), to perform end-to-end key-value extraction directly from document images, jointly solving identification, localization, and association in a single pass without OCR preprocessing. We extend DocTags with specialized key, value, region, and link tags, enabling many-to-many relationships in a unified output sequence. To address data limitations, we design an augmentation pipeline combining synthetic form filling and graph-based crops that preserve complete key-value subgraphs. We further introduce a layout-aware evaluation framework extending text matching with spatial bounding box verification. On FUNSD, XFUND, and a large-scale private dataset, our model outperforms larger zero-shot VLM baselines under layout-aware evaluation, while being 27 times smaller than Qwen2.5-VL (7B) and over 5 times faster at inference. The model weights will be released publicly after publication.

</details>


### [36] [RDANet: Relative Degradation Aware Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20870)
*Rui Liu,Jing Nie,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RDANet解决红外小目标检测中尺度变化和背景复杂导致的性能不稳定问题，通过MSAD模块保留目标结构，PGSM模块维持局部对比度，在三个基准上实现最优且更稳定的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法在面对目标尺度变化或场景背景差异时表现不稳定，主要缺陷在于特征下采样过程中未能同时有效保持目标结构和在背景变化下维持判别性的局部对比度，导致不同条件下检测性能不平衡。

Method: 提出相对退化感知网络（RDANet），包含两个核心模块：1）多尺度抗混叠下采样（MSAD），结合多尺度抗混叠滤波和像素折叠聚合，减少分辨率降低时的混叠效应以保留目标形状并抑制背景响应；2）原型引导跳接记忆（PGSM），从共享内存检索块级原型并自适应整合到当前表示中，以增强跳接特征并在多样背景中稳定局部对比度线索。
