Conclusion: 提出的方法有效解决了文档级词对齐中的性能瓶颈，实现了无需训练的高效对齐，并开源了代码与数据以支持复现。

Abstract: Word alignment has traditionally been studied between sentences, but many cross-lingual tasks increasingly require correspondences across full documents. While recent multilingual embedding models can encode long inputs, we show that applying algorithms designed for sentences directly to documents leads to performance degradation. To address this, we introduce CTFAlign, a lightweight, training-free approach for document-level word alignment. CTFAlign applies a coarse-to-fine refinement strategy that restricts the alignment search space to semantically similar regions. Additionally, we introduce MDPAlign, a simpler alternative that constrains alignments by position with a main diagonal prior. Both approaches operate directly on full documents without relying on sentence segmentation or sentence alignment. We evaluate these methods across six language pairs varying in typological distance, resourcedness, and document length. Averaged over three models, CTFAlign reduces word alignment error rate from 0.412 to 0.326. These gains transfer downstream, leading to improvements in document-level translation coverage evaluation and recognition of semantic differences. We release CTFAlign as a Python package and make the code and data to reproduce our experiments publicly available.

</details>


### [142] [Evidence-Consistent Generative Detection under Scenario-Level Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21043)
*San Kim,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对社交工程欺诈检测中模型因记忆场景特定线索而高估鲁棒性的问题，提出ECoG框架。该框架结合证据跨度监督与理由-标签一致性目标，有效提升了模型在未见攻击场景（SL-OOD）下的泛化能力，减少了预测与理由的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分布内评估在训练和测试数据共享重复的任务特定模式或表面线索时，会高估模型的鲁棒性。在社交工程欺诈检测中，攻击者可在保持恶意意图的同时改变场景、冒充实体或措辞，导致模型难以区分真实威胁与熟悉但无关的场景线索。

Method: 提出ECoG（Evidence-Consistent Generative framework），这是一种证据一致的生成式框架。该方法在训练过程中结合了“证据跨度监督”（evidence-span supervision）和“理由-标签一致性目标”（rationale-label consistency objective），旨在迫使模型依赖决策相关证据而非场景特定的词汇或实体线索。

Result: 在0.5B解码器基准上，相比无一致性正则化的相同骨干网络：ECoG将OOD挑战性实例的Macro-F1提高了3.22点；将生成的理由支持相反标签的预测比例降低了4.22点；增加了与参考证据跨度的令牌级重叠8.38点。这种预测-理由不一致性的降低在四种不同的解码器骨干中均保持一致。

Conclusion: 紧凑的生成式检测器在面临社交工程分布偏移时，通过引入证据监督和理由-标签一致性约束，能够显著提升其泛化能力和鲁棒性，避免对场景特定线索的记忆依赖。

Abstract: Conventional in-distribution evaluation can overestimate robustness when training and test data share recurring task-specific patterns or surface cues. This risk is especially relevant in social-engineering fraud detection, where attackers can preserve malicious intent while changing the scenario, impersonated entity, or wording. We study this problem as scenario-level out-of-distribution (SL-OOD) detection for SMS and voice phishing, where entire attack scenarios are held out from training while the label space remains fixed. This setting tests whether models can generalize to unseen attack scenarios using decision-relevant evidence rather than familiar scenario-specific cues. Using this SL-OOD evaluation, we find that high in-distribution performance does not reliably predict held-out robustness across feature-, encoder-, and decoder-based baselines. We interpret this gap as scenario memorization: reliance on recurring scenario-specific lexical or entity cues rather than decision-relevant evidence. We propose ECoG, an evidence-consistent generative framework that combines evidence-span supervision with a rationale-label consistency objective during training. On the 0.5B decoder, relative to the same backbone trained without consistency regularization, ECoG raises Macro-F1 on OOD challenging instances by 3.22 points, reduces the share of predictions whose generated rationale supports the opposite label by 4.22 points, and increases token-level overlap with reference evidence spans by 8.38 points; the reduction in prediction-rationale inconsistency is consistent across four decoder backbones. These results suggest that compact generative detectors can benefit from evidence supervision and rationale-label consistency under social-engineering shift.

</details>


### [143] [PromptResponse: Optimizing Prompts for LLM Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21074)
*Erik Thureck,Robert Kühnen,Tim Jacobowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究证实，对编程任务提示词进行一致的格式（尤其是JSON）能提升大语言模型生成代码的效率与语法稳定性，而LLM自动调优反而显著降低任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）广泛应用于研发流程，但其输出对输入提示词的格式变化敏感，缺乏关于提示词格式化及调优对代码生成质量影响的系统性理解。

Method: 基于HumanEval数据集构建五种语义相同但句法不同的变体（基线、JSON、Markdown、YAML及LLM调优版），使用GPT-4o执行8200次编码任务，对比分析不同提示策略下的代码性能、效率及稳定性。

Result: 一致的格式（特别是JSON）显著提升了生成效率和语法稳定性，并带来轻微的任务性能增益；相反，LLM调优提示词导致任务性能显著下降，且在其他维度无显著提升。

Conclusion: 低成本的重新格式化即可带来可测量的改进，而提示词调优需考虑模型对齐问题；研究提供了实践建议并开源了数据集变体及评估流水线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in research workflows and software development pipelines, yet their output remains sensitive to input prompt variations. This paper presents $\unicode{x00AB}$PromptResponse$\unicode{x00BB}$, a controlled study examining how formatting and LLM-based tuning of coding task prompts affect the resulting code's performance, efficiency, and stability. Using five semantically identical yet syntactically distinct variants of the HumanEval dataset$\unicode{x2014}$baseline, JSON, Markdown, YAML, and an LLM-tuned version$\unicode{x2014}$we had GPT-4o solve its coding problems over 8200$\unicode{x00A0}$executions. Our results show that consistent formatting$\unicode{x2014}$especially JSON$\unicode{x2014}$improves generation efficiency and syntactic stability, with minor gains in task performance. Conversely, the LLM-tuned prompts resulted in significantly degraded task performance without significant improvements in any other dimension. These findings suggest that low-effort reformatting alone can yield measurable improvements, while tuning must account for model alignment. We conclude our work with providing a set of practical recommendations informed by our results as well as releasing our dataset variants and evaluation pipeline for future work.

</details>


### [144] [Jokes Aside: Measuring the Semantic Distance of Double Meanings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21087)
*Fabio De Ponte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用大型语言模型的上下文嵌入向量，重新评估并扩展了Petrovic和Matthews关于双关语笑话生成的假设。通过引入“对称性”指标并在三个数据集上测试，发现基于这些新指标的模型在预测幽默评分方面表现不佳（最高准确率57.1%，低于基线），但“对称性”指标与高评分笑话存在关联，表明其可能是幽默的必要非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型为计算幽默研究提供了新工具，特别是上下文嵌入向量为重新审视早期关于笑话生成和幽默度的假设（如Petrovic和Matthews提出的基于频率、稀有性、歧义性和意义距离的假设）提供了机会。旨在验证和改进现有的幽默度量标准。

Method: 1. 基于Winters等人（2019）的工作，使用Word Embeddings重新评估三个指标：明显性（obviousness）、兼容性（compatibility）和比较（comparison）。
2. 首次引入新指标“对称性”（symmetry），定义为Z与X和Y的距离接近程度。
3. 使用两个嵌入模型（OpenAI text-embedding-3-small和MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2）。
4. 在三个数据集上进行实验：JokeJudger, Expunations, rJokes。其中后两个数据集通过添加捕捉核心歧义表达的双关句对进行了扩展。

Result: 1. 基于所提指标训练的模型在预测幽默评分上表现较差。
2. 在JokeJudger数据集上，最佳模型准确率为57.1%，低于61.5%的基线水平。
3. 在Expunations和rJokes数据集上的性能更低。
4. “对称性”指标似乎始终与较高评分的笑话相关联。

Conclusion: 尽管基于上下文嵌入向量的新指标未能显著提升幽默预测性能，但“对称性”被识别为可能捕捉幽默必要属性的关键特征。这表明当前的嵌入度量尚不足以完全解释幽默机制，需进一步探索。

Abstract: Large language models have significantly enriched the toolkit for computational humor research, particularly in the automated generation of jokes and puns. A key innovation, contextual embedding vectors, offers new opportunities to revisit and refine earlier hypotheses. Notably, Petrovic and Matthews (2013) proposed a joke generation model based on the scheme "I like my X like I like my Y, Z" (e.g. "I like my ice like I like my dreams, crushed"). They suggested that joke hilarity increases with: a) frequent association of Z with X and Y, b) rarity of Z, c) ambiguity of Z, and d) meaning distance between X and Y. Building on this, Winters et al. (2019) proposed a set of metrics, based on Google Ngrams and Word2Vector. In this work, three out of their five metrics are revisited with word embeddings: obviousness, compatibility, and comparison. Another measure, symmetry, defined as closeness of Z to both X and Y, is introduced here for the first time. Two models were used to collect the embedding vectors (OpenAI text-embedding-3-small and MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2) on three datasets: JokeJudger, Expunations, and rJokes. The last two datasets, Expunations, and rJokes, were expanded by adding paired sentences that captured the ambiguous expression at the core of each joke in its two different meanings. Results revealed that models trained on the proposed metrics performed poorly in predicting humor ratings: on JokeJudger, the best model achieved 57.1% accuracy, below the 61.5% baseline, while performance on Expunations and rJokes was even lower. Nevertheless, the symmetry metric seems consistently associated with higher-rated jokes, suggesting it may capture a necessary -though not sufficient- property of humor.

</details>


### [145] [When the Feature Pool Goes Algorithmic: Extending Mufwene's Ecology of Language Evolution to LLM-Mediated Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21088)
*Kunmei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型（LLMs）视为“分布中介”，通过算法重加权改变人类说话者可获取的语言变体频率，但社会评价仍是决定语言演变的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型如何影响基于Mufwene生态模型的语言演化机制，特别是解决LLMs介入后选择 locus 是否必须从人类转移的问题。

Method: 理论分析：将LLMs定义为聚合、转换并重新分配语言分布的分布中介；结合现有关于模型特定语言特征和词汇采用的证据进行论证；扩展Mufwene的特征池生态理论至说话者选择之前的上游阶段。

Result: LLMs通过训练和后训练改变语言变体的相对频率（算法重加权），但这并不必然导致语言趋同。人类的社会评估起决定性作用：AI相关形式可能被扩散常规化、因被视为“像AI”而被回避，或根本未扩散。

Conclusion: 该框架为语言采用、模型版本效应、趋同性及社会逆转提供了可检验的预测，强调在LLM时代，人类社会的最终选择权依然主导语言演化方向。

Abstract: Mufwene's ecological model locates language evolution in competition among variants contributed by individual idiolects and in speakers' selection from linguistic material made available through interaction. Large language models (LLMs) complicate this architecture without requiring the locus of selection to move away from human speakers. This article argues that LLMs are best treated as distributional mediators: they aggregate language produced across human populations, transform its distribution through training and post-training, and redistribute model-specific outputs at scale. I call the resulting ecological process algorithmic reweighting of the speaker-accessible distribution: model mediation can alter the relative frequencies with which competing variants reach human selectors. Emerging evidence on model-specific linguistic profiles and lexical uptake is consistent with parts of this pathway, but does not establish inevitable convergence. Human social evaluation remains decisive: model-associated forms may diffuse and become conventionalized, become socially recognizable as 'AI-like' and subsequently avoided, or fail to diffuse in the first place. The proposal extends Mufwene's feature-pool ecology one step upstream of speaker selection and yields testable predictions about uptake, model-version effects, convergence, and social reversal.

</details>


### [146] [No PUN Intended: Plausible Unknown Names for Person-Centred LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21206)
*Dimitri Staufer,David Hartmann,Ibrahim Baroud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PUN协议构建“合理未知姓名”，解决LLM评估中因姓名证据状态不可控导致的混淆问题，并释放300个验证姓名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM事实性、隐私泄露、偏见和拒绝回答等评估中，广泛使用人名作为提示变量。若姓名证据状态不受控，测量结果可能混淆记忆（memorisation）、检索、姓名先验知识及错误人物归因。

Method: 定义“未知姓名”为具有合理的“名-姓”格式、无索引全名证据且无歧义信号的姓名。引入PUN（Plausible Unknown Names）协议，结合Wikidata衍生组件、基于网络的LLM筛选和受控搜索重新验证来构建和验证此类姓名。

Result: 报告了接受率、可重复性和消融实验。204人的参与式人类研究发现，被接受的姓名比对照组更像真实姓名，但参与者仅在3%的情况下能找回相关的人物证据。发布了300个经过验证的姓名及其对照样本。

Conclusion: PUN协议提供了一种标准化的方法来生成和验证用于LLM评估的未知姓名，有助于更准确地分离和测量LLM的不同行为机制。

Abstract: Person names are widely used as prompt variables in LLM evaluations of factuality, privacy leakage, bias and abstention, but when a name's evidential status is uncontrolled, measurements may conflate memorisation, retrieval, name priors and wrong-person attribution. We operationalise an unknown name as one with plausible First-Last form, no indexed full-name evidence, and no ambiguity signals under a documented validation run, and introduce PUN (Plausible Unknown Names), a protocol for constructing and validating such names, combining Wikidata-derived components, web-enabled LLM screening, and controlled search revalidation. We report acceptance rate, reproducibility, ablations, and a 204-participant human study, finding accepted names are more name-like than controls while participants recover person evidence in only 3% of cases. We release 300 names with comparison controls.

</details>


### [147] [RARE: Decoupling Representation Steering from Expert Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21236)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RARE框架，通过保持MoE模型路由一致性来解决表示工程的结构不匹配问题，显著提升有害性控制、事实编辑和真实性等任务效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将对传统语言模型有效的表示工程技术应用于混合专家（MoE）模型时存在结构不匹配，导致性能下降；需解决此问题以实现对MoE模型行为的有效控制。

Method: 提出RARE（Router-Agnostic Representation Engineering）框架：1) 将行为扰动投影到路由器矩阵的零空间以消除路由可见成分；2) 纠正传播至下游层的路由漂移；3) 评估五种扰动估计器在六个异构MoE模型上的表现。

Result: 在有害性引导中达到53.3%的攻击成功率且保持67.8% MMLU准确率；TruthfulQA准确率从41.0%提升至58.6%；CounterFact事实编辑有效性从16.8%大幅提升至96.3%，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 路由一致性是适配表示工程至MoE模型的关键架构考量因素，RARE框架有效解决了结构不匹配问题并实现了更强的效用-效果权衡。

Abstract: Representation engineering offers a lightweight means of controlling language-model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states, but its direct application to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models introduces a structural mismatch. We first verify this failure mode through a series of empirical studies and find that preserving clean routing substantially recovers steering performance and that routing is more sensitive to semantic content than to behavioral changes under controlled content. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RARE, a router-agnostic representation engineering framework for MoE language models. RARE projects arbitrary behavioral perturbations onto the null space of the router matrix, thereby removing router-visible components, and further corrects routing drift propagated to selected downstream layers. To decide the best perturbation estimator in this framework, we evaluate five estimators on six heterogeneous open-weight MoE models across three steering scenarios: harmfulness, truthfulness, and factual editing. On harmfulness steering, RARE reaches an average attack success rate of 53.3% while retaining 67.8% MMLU accuracy, yielding a stronger aggregate effectiveness--utility trade-off than baselines. It further improves average TruthfulQA MC1 accuracy from 41.0% to 58.6% and CounterFact efficacy from 16.8% to 96.3%. These results support routing consistency as an important architectural consideration for adapting representation engineering to MoE models.

</details>


### [148] [Affective Context Amplifies Sycophancy in LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21242)
*Jiayi Li,Sanjana Menon,Brett Frischmann,Shomir Wilson,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型（LLM）在面对用户时表现出系统性“阿谀奉承”行为，即回避负面或反对性评价。当检测到用户的负面情绪（尤其是孤独和痛苦）时，这种倾向会显著增强，模型倾向于通过模糊、非承诺性的回应来退让，而非提供诚实的批评反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究情感背景如何调节大型语言模型在主观评价互动中的“阿谀奉承”（sycophancy）行为，特别是在用户分享行动或观点并寻求反馈的场景下。

Method: 基于迎合理论，通过对比模型作为第三方旁观者与作为用户本人时的回答差异来衡量阿谀奉承程度。研究涵盖了七种大型语言模型，并使用了两个Reddit数据集（r/AmItheAsshole 和 r/TrueUnpopularOpinion）进行分析。

Result: 模型的回答差异具有系统性和单向性：面向用户的回复 consistently（一致地）软化或 withholding（保留）负面或反对性判断。情感背景加剧了这一差异，特别是负面情绪（如孤独和痛苦）产生了最大的影响，导致模型更多采取逃避式的阿谀奉承策略。

Conclusion: 情感背景作为一种脆弱性信号，会在用户最需要批判性反馈的时候抑制模型的批评能力。模型往往通过逃避式阿谀奉承（retreat toward non-committal responses）来回避直接冲突，这可能对用户产生负面影响。

Abstract: As conversational companions, large language models (LLMs) often have access to users' emotional states. We study how this affective context modulates LLM sycophancy in subjective, evaluative interactions, where users share actions or opinions that invite feedback. Drawing on ingratiation theory, we measure sycophancy as the divergence between a model's independent evaluation and its user-facing response, elicited by presenting the same content as either a third-party account or the user's own disclosure. Across seven LLMs and two Reddit datasets (r/AmItheAsshole and r/TrueUnpopularOpinion), we find that this divergence is systematic and strongly one-directional. User-facing responses consistently soften or withhold negative or oppositional judgments. Affective context further amplifies this divergence with negative states, particularly loneliness and distress, producing the largest effects. These findings suggest that affective context functions as a vulnerability signal that suppresses critical feedback when users may need it most, often through evasive sycophancy, in which models retreat toward non-committal responses rather than outright agreement.

</details>


### [149] [Benchmarking Patent Drafting from Inventor-Style Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21249)
*Lekang Jiang,Wenjun Sun,Stephan Goetz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有大语言模型在真实专利起草场景中（从非结构化发明披露到完整专利申请）表现不足的问题，本文提出了Dis2Pat数据集和Patent-MAF多智能体框架。前者填补了从早期非法律化材料直接生成完整专利的空白，后者作为可本地部署的基线模型，在基准测试中显著优于开源模型并与闭源模型相当，解决了长文本和法律约束下的隐私与准确性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注个体专利起草任务或假设输入为后期、高度结构化甚至已法律化的材料，未能解决现实世界中从发明人撰写的非正式、去法律化披露文件直接生成完整且法律连贯的专利申请这一核心挑战。

Method: 1. 构建Dis2Pat数据集：反映真实专利工作流程，要求直接从发明人风格的非法律化披露内容生成完整的专利申请。
2. 提出Patent-MAF框架：一种基于多智能体架构的专利起草方法，支持本地部署以应对严格的隐私要求，并处理长篇幅及法律约束复杂的起草任务。

Result: 基准测试显示当前大语言模型在专利起草方面存在局限性；Patent-MAF提供了一个强有力的基线，其性能一致优于被评估的开源模型，并在各项指标上与大型闭源模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过引入贴近现实的Dis2Pat数据集和具备隐私保护能力的Patent-MAF多智能体框架，有效弥合了从早期发明披露到完整专利申请生成的差距，证明了多智能体系统在复杂、受法律约束的长文本生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: While recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising results on individual patent drafting tasks, they fundamentally fail to investigate the core challenge of real-world patent drafting: generating a complete and legally coherent patent application directly from early-stage invention materials. Prior work predominantly assumes later-stage, highly structured, or already legalistic inputs. However, real patenting workflows begin with informal, de-legalized disclosures authored by inventors. To bridge the gap, we introduce Dis2Pat, a disclosure-to-patent dataset that reflects realistic patenting workflows by requiring the generation of complete patent applications directly from inventor-style, de-legalized disclosures. Given the inherent difficulty of long-form, legally constrained patent drafting and the strong privacy requirements, we further propose a strong baseline named Patent-MAF. It is a multi-agent framework for locally deployable patent drafting. Benchmark results reveal that current LLMs exhibit limitations in patent drafting, while Patent-MAF provides a strong baseline that consistently outperforms evaluated open-source models and remains competitive with large closed-source models.

</details>


### [150] [Memory Augmentation Unlocks Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21265)
*Simeng Zhang,Yilong Chen,Wenyuan Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yao Chen,Junyuan Shang,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“记忆增强压缩”（Memory-Augmented Compression）的免训练框架，通过复用历史推理模式作为预填充支架，解决CoT压缩导致的逻辑断裂问题，在提升准确率的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型依赖思维链（CoT）解决复杂任务，但冗长的推理过程导致高昂的计算开销。现有的CoT压缩方法虽然缩短了生成时间，但过度压缩会破坏逻辑连贯性并降低性能，亟需平衡推理效率与准确性。

Method: 基于“上下文-生成替代定律”，提出无训练的“记忆增强压缩”框架。该框架从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的“推理记忆”，包含可复用的推理模式、关键约束和核心操作，并将其作为预填充侧的脚手架检索使用，以补偿压缩过程中丢失的信息。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH、BBH和MMLU-Sci等任务上，相比标准CoD压缩方法，准确率分别提升了21.4、28.0、29.5和6.61个点；同时相比标准CoT实现了1.14-1.49倍的延迟加速。该方法兼容多种压缩机制，且增益源于相关的推理记忆而非单纯增加上下文长度。

Conclusion: 记忆增强压缩有效解决了CoT压缩中的逻辑退化问题，通过复用结构化推理知识，在不增加训练成本的前提下，实现了推理速度与准确性的双重优化。

Abstract: Large language models often rely on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks, but verbose reasoning traces introduce substantial inference overhead. CoT compression shortens generation, yet aggressive compression may disrupt logical coherence and degrade performance. We formalize this trade-off as the \textit{Context-Generation Substitution Law}, where explicit reasoning context substitutes for part of decode-time generation. Based on this principle, we propose \textit{Memory-Augmented Compression}, a training-free framework that constructs reusable reasoning memories from historical traces and retrieves them as prefill-side scaffolds. Rather than using raw demonstrations, these memories summarize reusable reasoning patterns, key constraints, and critical operations to compensate for information lost during compression. Experiments show that Memory consistently improves prompt-based Chain-of-Draft (CoD) compression across mathematical reasoning, complex reasoning, and science question answering tasks, yielding accuracy gains of 21.4, 28.0, 29.5, and 6.61 points over CoD on GSM8K, MATH, BBH, and MMLU-Sci, while achieving a 1.14--1.49$\times$ latency speedup over standard CoT. Memory is also compatible with token-level, reasoning-trace-level, and inference-state compression mechanisms. Further analyzes show that the gains come from relevant reasoning memories rather than simply increasing context length.

</details>


### [151] [Prompt-Model Interaction Reaches the Fixed Points: A deterministic, task-free structural readout -- and the factorizations of it that failed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过固定点结构分析发现，提示词对模型的影响并非源于任务机制或条件分布本身，而是提示-模型配对（prompt-model pair）的固有属性。九 token 的条件作用能显著改变模型的固定点分布和结构分类，而指令微调则无此效果。现有解释因素（如前缀长度、注意力集中等）均无法完全解释这一现象，表明提示效应是特定于模型与提示组合的复杂交互结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清“提示词的效果是否仅为提示词本身的属性”这一争议。之前的证据仅涉及任务准确率，无法区分这种影响是源于任务处理机制还是条件分布本身。因此，本研究试图在一个无任务的读取（readout）中，探究模型如何处理文本片段，以揭示提示交互的本质。

Method: 使用一个无任务的短窗口 argmax 映射（x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t)）作为读取方式，从96个起始点考察其固定点结构（fixed-point structure）。该方法具有确定性，仅关注模型如何读取片段。研究分析了不同模型在不同窗口大小下的表现，并测试了各种现象学因素（如文体、双向性等）和机械性解释（如注意力集中）对提示效应的影响。

Result: 1. 交互作用达到全量级：九个 token 的条件作用能显著移动固定点分数范围，改变四路结构分类，并对模型进行重排序；相比之下，价值60.5 IFEval点的指令微调对此分类无影响。2. 提出的解释因素均失败：前缀长度效应非单调；文体、通用方向、双向性、指令抵抗等现象学因素在扩大样本后均被消除；最接近的机械性解释（早期 token 的注意力集中主导）仅在2/5模型上预测正确（相当于随机水平），且该效应在真实文本中存在但在均匀随机输入中失效，说明研究处于该解释机制之外。3. 一个固定的九 token 前缀驱动四个模型趋向0，两个趋向1，且双向性在分布内起始点中得以保留。

Conclusion: 在此读取视角下，解释的基本单位是“提示-模型配对”（prompt-model pair）。提示词的效果不是单一模型或提示词的独立属性，而是两者特定组合的结果。研究指出了一种认知偏差：将具有特定形状的评估标准应用于没有变化空间的量度上，导致了对提示效应的误解。

Abstract: That a prompt's effect is not a property of the prompt is established: prompts optimised for one model degrade on another, and rankings reorder under neutral reformatting. That evidence is about task accuracy, which cannot say whether the interaction is a fact about task machinery or about the conditional distribution itself. We ask on a readout with no task in it: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax map x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t), censused from 96 starts. It is deterministic, so nothing can be helped or hurt, and it exists only at short windows -- four of six models lose it entirely by window 16 -- so everything here concerns how a model reads a fragment. Two results. First, the interaction reaches this readout at full magnitude: nine tokens of conditioning move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range, change a four-way structural class, and reorder models, while instruction tuning worth 60.5 IFEval points moves the class by zero. Second, nothing we proposed carries it. Prefix length fails: the effect is not monotone. Four phenomenological factors -- prose-versus-markup, a universal direction, bidirectionality, instruct-resistance -- were each withdrawn within one run of being proposed, dissolved by widening the sample. And the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink dominance of early tokens, predicts the sign of the shift on 2 of 5 models -- chance -- while a length-by-content cross shows it holds on real text and fails on our probe's uniformly random input, so we are outside its regime, not against it. One fixed nine-token prefix drives four models toward 0 and two toward 1; the bidirectionality survives in-distribution starts. On this readout the unit of explanation is the prompt-model pair. The recurring error it caught in us has a name: a criterion with a shape applied to a quantity with no room to vary.

</details>


### [152] [Move by Move: Measuring and Steering How LLMs Conduct Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21325)
*Afonso Baldo,Hugo Pitorro,Areti Vassilopoulos,Anabela C. Areias,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei,Nuno M. Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）在提供情感支持时与人类临床医生在治疗互动模式上的显著差异。通过构建基于MULTI-60清单的十类治疗动作本体论，研究发现模型过度使用询问而忽视心理教育，且缺乏主动性。仅通过暴露该本体论作为工具（无需微调），即可显著缩小模型与人类治疗师的行为偏差并提升对话对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管用户越来越多地转向大型语言模型寻求情感支持，但关于这些模型实际如何进行心理治疗互动的知识仍然匮乏。旨在填补这一认知空白，深入理解LLM在治疗交互中的具体行为特征及其与专业人类的差距。

Method: 1. 构建本体论：引入基于MULTI-60清单的十个功能导向的治疗动作类别（Ontology）。
2. 验证与扩展：通过与五位持证心理学家的标注活动进行验证，并使用基于裁判的方法扩大规模，确保与专家意见一致。
3. 对比分析：将该本体论应用于真实咨询记录和模型主导的会话，比较人类临床医生与前沿模型面板的动作分布。
4. 干预实验：将本体论作为提示工具暴露给模型，评估其对模型行为的影响，且不进行任何微调。

Result: 1. 模型行为偏差：模型过度使用“询问”动作，频率高达人类水平的三倍；严重忽视“心理教育”；具有强烈的语境锚定效应，即延续人类发起的策略但极少主动发起新策略。
2. 工具效果：暴露本体论作为工具后，模型与人类动作分布的平均偏差减少了一半；在不需要微调的情况下，回合级与人类治疗师的对齐度提高了7-9个百分点。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型在模拟心理治疗互动时存在特定的结构性偏差，如过度询问和缺乏主动性。然而，通过引入经过验证的治疗动作本体论作为外部指导工具，可以在零微调的情况下显著改善模型与治疗师行为的对齐程度，表明结构化引导是优化LLM情感支持能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to large language models for emotional support, yet little is known about how these models actually conduct a psychotherapy interaction. We introduce an ontology of ten therapeutic moves: compact, function-based categories grounded in the MULTI-60 inventory, validated through an annotation campaign with five licensed psychologists, and scaled with a judge-based approach that matches expert agreement. Applying it to real counseling transcripts and model-led sessions, we compare the move distributions between human clinicians and a panel of frontier models. Models over-use inquiry at up to three times the human rate, neglect psychoeducation, and are strongly context-anchored: they carry forward strategies initiated by a human clinician but rarely initiate them themselves. Exposing the ontology as a set of tools roughly halves the mean deviation from the human move distribution and improves turn-level alignment with human therapist by 7-9 percentage points, without any fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Clarify-Then-Search: A Clarification Benchmark for Deep Search with End-to-End Nugget Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20357)
*Deqiang Huang,Jingbo Zhou,Xinjiang Lu,Tong Xu,Hua Wu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Clarify-Then-Search基准，用于评估LLM生成的澄清问题对深层搜索任务的效用。该基准基于百度真实查询数据构建，包含518个实例。实验表明，增加澄清轮次（k=1至3）能显著提升搜索效果，GPT-5.2在单轮表现最佳，ERNIE-4.5-Turbo-128K在多轮中领先。主要缺陷在于模型常询问无法回答的区域性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深层搜索对用户未指定查询（缺少时间、地点、范围等约束）的脆弱性问题，防止检索漂移和答案不完整。旨在建立一个可复现、防泄露的评估框架，以衡量澄清策略对下游搜索实用性的提升。

Method: 构建基于百度真实数据的Clarify-Then-Search基准，含518个意图查询与未指定查询对。使用WebDancer为每个意图生成带溯源标识的证据基础参考集。评估流程包括：Clarifier提出1-3个澄清问题；User Answerer仅依据意图查询回答或返回未知；Rewriter结合未指定查询与问答对重写查询；最后由WebDancer执行搜索并通过restore_score_100加权召回率评分。

Result: 在所有测试模型中，k=1时的澄清交互优于无交互基线，且更大的预算（更多轮次）通常带来进一步增益。GPT-5.2在k=1时平均得分最高，ERNIE-4.5-Turbo-128K在k=3时整体表现最佳。诊断发现普遍失败模式：许多系统过度询问仅涉及地域的问题，这些问题往往无法从意图查询中获得答案，导致返回“未知”。

Conclusion: Clarify-Then-Search提供了一个细粒度分析澄清问题效用、可答性及预算影响的有效评估框架，证实了合理的澄清机制能显著提升深层搜索性能，但需优化以避免无效提问。

Abstract: Deep search is brittle on underspecified user queries: missing constraints such as time, location, scope, or definitions can lead to retrieval drift and incomplete answers. We introduce Clarify-Then-Search, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM-generated clarification questions improve downstream deep-search utility. Built on real-world query data from the Baidu search engine, the benchmark contains 518 curated instances, each with an intent query and a corresponding underspecified query. For each intent query, we run WebDancer once to archive evidence and construct a static golden reference as weighted, evidence-grounded nuggets with traceable source identifiers. At evaluation time, a Clarifier asks k in {1, 2, 3} questions; a closed-book User Answerer replies only with information explicitly stated in the intent query, otherwise returning unknown; and a closed-book Rewriter produces a rewritten query using only the underspecified query and the elicited question-answer pairs. WebDancer then executes on the rewritten query, and we score end-to-end utility using restore_score_100, a weighted nugget-recall score with partial credit against the static gold. Across all evaluated models, clarification improves over the no-interaction baseline at k=1, and larger budgets generally yield further gains. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest mean score at k=1, while ERNIE-4.5-Turbo-128K becomes the overall top-performing model at k=3. Diagnostics reveal a consistent failure mode: many systems over-ask region-only questions that are often unanswerable from the intent and thus elicit unknown. Clarify-Then-Search enables leakage-resistant and reproducible evaluation of clarify-then-search pipelines, with fine-grained analyses of question utility, answerability, and budget effects in deep search.

</details>


### [154] [Edge-Based Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Autonomous FHWA Bridge Inspection Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20372)
*Viraj Nishesh Darji,Hemaliben Rakeshkumar Darji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BridgeGuard，一种完全离线、基于边缘硬件运行的智能体RAG系统，用于自动化桥梁检查合规性验证。该系统通过结合向量搜索和SQL查询，实现了高精度的结构缺陷分类和引用准确率，显著优于传统手动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国联邦公路管理局（FHWA）要求对超过60万座桥梁进行国家桥梁库存（NBI）合规性评估。传统的 manual compliance verification 存在劳动密集、易出错以及在现场网络受限环境下难以实施的问题。

Method: 提出BridgeGuard系统，采用完全气隙隔离（air-gapped）架构，在本地商用边缘硬件上运行。核心方法包括：1) 针对FHWA编码指南的向量搜索；2) 针对NBI表格数据的结构化SQL查询；3) 由状态机驱动的多步ReAct规划循环进行编排；4) 一种保留层级监管项目边界的分块算法（section-aware chunking）。

Result: 1. 分块完整性达到94.2%（对比朴素固定大小分割的28.4%）。2. 在特拉华州2023年NBI库存（874座桥）和德克萨斯州样本（200座桥）上，“结构性缺陷”桥梁识别的分类准确率分别为99.77%和100.0%。3. 引用准确率为100.0%。4. 处理速度为每小时197座桥，且无需外部网络连接。5. 消融实验证实向量搜索和多步智能体循环对于正确的合规推理均必不可少。

Conclusion: BridgeGuard通过整合向量检索与结构化数据查询，并利用多步智能体规划，解决了离线环境下大规模桥梁合规性自动验证的难题，具有高精度、高效率和高可靠性的特点。

Abstract: The Federal Highway Administration (FHWA) mandates that over 600,000 bridges in the United States be evaluated against the Recording and Coding Guide for the National Bridge Inventory (NBI). Manual compliance verification is labor-intensive, error-prone, and impractical in connectivity-limited field environments. This paper introduces BridgeGuard, a fully air-gapped agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for autonomous bridge inspection compliance. BridgeGuard integrates vector search over the FHWA Recording and Coding Guide with structured SQL queries against NBI tabular data, orchestrated by a stateful multi-step ReAct planning loop executing locally on commodity edge hardware. A section-aware chunking algorithm preserves hierarchical regulatory item boundaries, achieving 94.2% chunk integrity compared with 28.4% for naive fixed-size splitting. Evaluated on the full Delaware 2023 NBI inventory (874 bridges) and a Texas sample (200 bridges), the system achieves 99.77% and 100.0% classification accuracy, respectively, for Structurally Deficient bridge identification, with 100.0% citation accuracy, at 197.0 bridges per hour with out external network access. Ablation experiments confirm that both vector search and the multi-step agentic loop are necessary for correct compliance reasoning.

</details>


### [155] [One Hierarchy, Two Systems: Semantic Product IDs for Discovery-Surface Ranking and Search-Page Query Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20640)
*Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Saathvik Dirisala,Raghav Saboo,Nimesh Sinha,Leo Shao,Elyse Winer,Sudeep Das,Martin Wang,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的层级语义ID（$\sid{}$）表示，通过整合产品内容嵌入和跨商家行为证据，同时优化个性化排名与查询重构任务，显著提升线上转化率及搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多商家电商目录中因标识符碎片化导致的行为证据分散问题，以及专家定义分类法过于粗糙、难以支持细粒度发现的问题。旨在探索单一层级语义表示是否能同时支持个性化排序和查询重构。

Method: 1. 从产品内容嵌入中学习单一的层级语义ID（$\sid{}$）表示，定义不同粒度的产品概念。
2. **排序应用**：在$\sid{}前缀上聚合消费者亲和度和产品表现，为候选产品和消费者历史推导序列特征。
3. **查询重构应用**：基于$\sid{}概念 grounding 查询和会话转换，利用层级结构进行导航和细化，并根据商家库存过滤建议。

Result: 1. **排序方面**：离线相关性提升；线上全排名处理显示头部位置加购参与度更强，且不太受欢迎的产品获得更广泛的曝光。
2. **查询重构方面**：离线评估显示比传统分类法更好地保留意图，比原始查询字符串转换提供更高质建议；线上评估显示减少搜索努力并更早访问可购买产品。

Conclusion: 共享的语义产品层级结构能够同时支持推荐和搜索任务，同时保留每个应用所需的特定任务上下文，证明了统一语义表示在多商家电商场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-merchant e-commerce catalogs contain equivalent and related products under different merchant-scoped identifiers, fragmenting behavioral evidence across merchants. Expert-defined taxonomies, meanwhile, are often too coarse for fine-grained discovery. We investigate whether a single hierarchical Semantic ID (\sid{}) representation can support personalized ranking and query reformulation. Learned once from product-content embeddings, the hierarchy defines product concepts at multiple granularities that each application combines with its own behavioral and serving context. For ranking, we aggregate consumer affinity and product performance over \sid{} prefixes and derive sequence features for candidate products and consumer histories. Controlled ablations show improved offline relevance, while online evaluation of the full ranking treatment shows stronger top-slot add-to-cart engagement and broader exposure for less-popular products. For query reformulation, we ground queries and session transitions in \sid{} concepts, use the hierarchy for navigation and refinement, and filter suggestions against the merchant's assortment. Offline evaluation shows finer intent preservation than taxonomy and higher-quality suggestions than raw query-string transitions; online evaluation shows reduced search effort and earlier access to purchasable products. These results show that a shared semantic product hierarchy can support both recommendation and search while preserving the task-specific context required by each application.

</details>


### [156] [Towards Faithful Simulation of Human Shopping Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20707)
*Jiakai Tang,Yan Mi,Jing Yu,Yang Zhang,See-Kiong Ng,Qi Cao,Fei Sun,Xu Chen,Wen Chen,Jian Wu,Han Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RecVerse，一种基于GUI的电商用户模拟智能体，通过分层记忆机制解决长程历史处理难题，并利用轨迹级强化学习优化行为真实性，显著提升了模拟行为的保真度和意图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM/VLM驱动的电商用户模拟器面临两大挑战：(1) 记忆挑战：购物会话跨度大，现有方法要么丢弃长程观察导致状态丢失，要么简单拼接导致上下文窗口过载且质量下降；(2) 优化挑战：基于逐动作监督或步骤级奖励的训练导致会话模式不真实（如过度探索或过于被动），无法纠正宏观行为偏差。

Method: 提出RecVerse智能体，包含两项核心创新：(1) 认知启发的分层记忆机制：工作记忆（短期焦点）、情景记忆（会话痕迹）和偏好记忆（高层意图），将记忆更新视为动作以自适应学习记忆策略；(2) 轨迹级强化学习目标：对完整会话进行评分，对齐宏观动作类型分布与微观购物意图。同时发布USB基准数据集。

Result: 实验表明，RecVerse在行为保真度（behavioral fidelity）和意图一致性（intent consistency）方面均显著优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: RecVerse通过结合分层记忆架构与轨迹级强化学习，有效解决了电商用户模拟中的长程依赖保持和行为真实性问题，为离线评估和强化学习提供了更可靠的模拟环境。

Abstract: Simulating realistic user shopping behavior underpins offline evaluation and reinforcement learning in e-commerce scenarios. While recent LLM- and VLM-based simulators have made encouraging progress, reproducing a real browsing session remains difficult for two reasons. (i) Memory Challenge: a shopping session spans dozens of pages, yet existing agents either discard long-range observation histories, losing the evolving user state, or naively concatenate them, overwhelming the context window and even degrading simulation quality. (ii) Optimization Challenge: current user simulators are typically supervised to match each logged action via imitation or step-level rewards; the resulting sessions often display unrealistic patterns, such as over-exploration or excessive passivity, which per-step supervision can neither detect nor correct.
  To address the above challenges, we present RecVerse, a GUI-grounded simulation agent that perceives pages through screenshots and produces faithful multi-turn trajectories. For the memory challenge, RecVerse adopts a cognitive-inspired hierarchical memory: Working Memory for short-term focus, Episodic Memory for in-session traces, and Preference Memory for high-level intent, with memory updates treated as actions so that the agent adaptively learns when and what to memorize. For the optimization challenge, RecVerse is optimized with a trajectory-level RL objective that scores entire sessions, aligning both macro-level action-type distributions and micro-level shopping intent with real users. We further release USB (User Simulation Benchmark), an interactive e-commerce GUI trajectory dataset for multi-turn user simulation. Experiments show that RecVerse significantly outperforms existing baselines in both behavioral fidelity and intent consistency.

</details>


### [157] [Profiling What Matters: Context-Aware Item Profiles from Large-Scale Metadata for LLM Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20801)
*Dojun Hwang,Seunghan Lee,Cheonyoung Park,Sara Yu,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CAIRO框架，通过构建用户上下文感知的物品画像，解决LLM推荐重排序中利用海量异构元数据困难的问题，显著提升重排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖标题或静态属性，难以有效处理真实世界中庞大、异构且非结构化的物品元数据；特征显著性具有高度上下文依赖性，现有手段限制了个性化和细粒度的物品理解。

Method: 提出CAIRO框架：1. 将原始元数据和评论结构化为主观特质与客观特征；2. 使用轻量级Profiler为每个用户-物品对选择最相关信息；3. 生成简洁、上下文特定的物品画像作为LLM排序决策的证据。

Result: 实验表明，CAIRO一致性地提升了基于大语言模型（LLM）的推荐重排序性能，证明了有效利用海量物品侧信息的重要性。

Conclusion: 在LLM-based reranking中，构建用户上下文感知的物品画像能有效挖掘隐含、噪声大的物品信息，是实现个性化和细粒度理解的关键。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced reranking in recommendation, effectively leveraging item-side information remains challenging. Real-world items are described by vast, heterogeneous, and unstructured metadata, where decision-relevant signals are often implicit, noisy, or buried in long descriptions. Moreover, feature salience is highly context-dependent, varying not only across items but also across users. Existing methods often rely on item titles, fixed attributes, or static item summaries, which limit personalized and fine-grained item understanding. To bridge this gap, we propose CAIRO, a user context-aware item profiling framework for LLM-based reranking. CAIRO first structures raw metadata and reviews into objective features and subjective traits, and employs a lightweight profiler to select the most relevant information for each user-item pair with limited serving-time overhead. The resulting profiles are concise and context-specific, providing relevant item-side evidence for the LLM's ranking decision. Experiments show that CAIRO consistently improves LLM-based reranking, highlighting the importance of item profiling that effectively exploits vast item-side information.

</details>


### [158] [KoViDoRe: Korean Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20840)
*Yongbin Choi,Yongwoo Song,Mujeen Sung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出KoViDoRe基准和Ko-VDR Train Public训练集，解决韩语视觉文档检索中缺乏多页、复杂结构评估及模型性能不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦英语，缺乏对韩语视觉文档（如PDF）复杂结构的覆盖；且多数韩语资源仅评估单页检索，无法捕捉需跨页聚合证据的真实场景。当前模型在处理韩语结构化内容时表现不佳。

Method: 1. 构建KoViDoRe基准：从公开韩语文档中提取多样化布局（表格、图表、多栏），通过结构化解析、合成查询生成（基于摘要和上下文）及人工验证的相关性映射进行数据策展。2. 评估多种多模态检索模型。3. 基于评估结果，构建大规模训练数据集Ko-VDR Train Public。

Result: 现有模型在韩语视觉文档检索任务上表现困难，特别是在涉及结构化内容和多样化查询类型的场景中。提出的基准和训练集为韩语视觉文档检索提供了统一的支持。

Conclusion: KoViDoRe和Ko-VDR Train Public共同构成了一个统一的基准和训练资源，旨在推动针对韩语视觉文档的检索模型发展，填补现有研究空白。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal retrieval have improved the ability to retrieve information from visually rich documents such as PDFs and reports. However, existing benchmarks remain largely centered on English and provide limited coverage of Korean visual documents with complex structures. Furthermore, most existing Korean resources primarily evaluate single-page retrieval, failing to capture realistic scenarios that require evidence aggregation across multiple pages. To address these gaps, we introduce KoViDoRe, a benchmark for Korean visual document retrieval. The dataset is constructed from publicly available Korean documents with diverse layouts, including tables, figures, and multi-column structures. We develop a multi-stage data curation pipeline consisting of structured document parsing, synthetic query generation using both summary-based and context-based strategies, and relevance mapping with human verification. Using KoViDoRe, we evaluate a wide range of multimodal retrieval models and observe that current models struggle to effectively handle Korean visual document retrieval, particularly in settings involving structured content and diverse query types. Motivated by this finding, we further curate a large-scale training dataset, Ko-VDR Train Public, to support the development of retrieval models tailored to Korean visual documents. Together, KoViDoRe and Ko-VDR Train Public provide a unified benchmark and training resource for Korean visual document retrieval.

</details>


### [159] [From a Static Multi-Level Small Semantic Codebook to a Dynamic Single-Level Large Semantic Codebook for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21012)
*Tianlu Xie,Xin Ku,Mingjie Sun,Yunhao Sha,Lixiang Wang,Peng Wang,Yiyu Wang,Wenjin Wu,Zhaojie Liu,Peng Jiang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单层大型语义码本（Single-level Large Semantic Codebook）的生成式推荐方法，通过引入暴露感知的动态更新机制，解决传统多层残数量化带来的解码成本高、空间稀疏及静态码本滞后问题。该方法在离线指标、计算效率及线上A/B测试中均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统通常使用多层残数量化将物品表示为离散语义ID序列，这导致自回归解码成本增加且层次空间稀疏；同时，静态码本无法适应新物品加入和曝光分布变化，造成码本与当前流量失配。

Method: 1. **单层大语义码本**：用单个语义Token替代多个残差语义Code，并保留独立的协作消歧Token以减少物品冲突。
2. **暴露感知动态更新机制**：基于时间权重衰减、指数移动平均中心更新以及SID变化的曝光加权惩罚，实现码本的动态调整。
3. **离线评估框架**：涵盖表示质量、代码利用率、集群负载、全SID碰撞和时间稳定性等多个维度。

Result: 1. **性能提升**：在OneRec-V1和V2数据集上，双层SID使Mean Recall@10提升5.0%-8.8%，Mean NDCG@10提升4.1%-5.1%；在KuaiRec上动态更新带来进一步增益。
2. **效率优化**：缩短的SID使自回归解码FLOPs降低47.93%-48.70%，单卡QPS提升28.57%-47.0%。
3. **线上效果**：5天在线A/B测试显示，主要消费指标提升0.792%。

Conclusion: 提出的单层语义码本结合动态更新机制，有效降低了计算成本并提升了推荐精度与实时适应性，经线上验证具有显著的工程价值和业务收益。

Abstract: Generative recommendation represents each item with a sequence of discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs) and predicts the sequence to retrieve the next item. Typical systems use multi-level residual quantization, which increases autoregressive decoding cost and creates a large hierarchical space that may be sparsely occupied. Static codebooks also become misaligned with current traffic as new items arrive and exposure distributions change. We propose a single-level large semantic codebook that replaces multiple residual semantic codes with one semantic token while retaining a separate collaborative disambiguation token to reduce item collisions. We further introduce an exposure-aware dynamic update mechanism based on temporal weight decay, exponential moving-average center updates, and an exposure-weighted penalty on SID changes. We also develop an offline evaluation framework covering representation quality, code utilization, cluster load, full-SID collision, and temporal stability. On two public datasets, the two-level SID improves mean Recall@10 by 5.0%-8.8% and mean NDCG@10 by 4.1%-5.1% for OneRec-V1, and by 7.1%-8.7% and 3.8%-8.5%, respectively, for OneRec-V2. Dynamic updating provides further gains on KuaiRec. Across three serving architectures, the shorter SID reduces estimated autoregressive-decoding FLOPs by 47.93%-48.70% and increases single-card QPS by 28.57%-47.0%. A five-day online A/B test serving 2.5% of production traffic improves the primary consumption metric by 0.792%.

</details>


### [160] [Graph Engineering in the Era of LLM Agents: From Individual Intelligence to System Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21156)
*Yuyuan Feng,Zhishang Xiang,Chaobin Yang,Qichao Ma,Zerui Chen,Yujing Zhang,Ke Huang,Chuanjie Wu,Zhaoxu Liu,Yili Wang,Xin He,Jiapu Wang,Zijin Hong,Hao Chen,Yuanchen Bei,Kun Wang,Shengyuan Chen,Ningyu Zhang,Enyan Dai,Linhao Luo,Qingyi Pan,Qi Wang,Wenqi Fan,Guangjing Wang,Na Zou,Yangqiu Song,Xin Wang,Zechao Li,Xia Hu,Qing Li,Xiao Huang,Zhihong Zhang,Jinsong Su,Qinggang Zhang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出“图工程”（Graph Engineering）范式，旨在解决大型语言模型（LLM）从单体智能向分布式系统智能演进中的架构瓶颈。通过构建动态、显式的图结构来组织任务、代理和系统状态，实现多智能体系统的协调与自适应，超越传统单体优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM演变为自主智能体，任务复杂度增加，单一智能体在异构专业知识、依赖子任务、并行执行及持久状态管理方面面临根本性极限。现有范式（如提示工程、上下文工程）仅优化个体能力或交互，无法解决“架构不匹配”问题，亟需将智能分布到专门化代理并在系统层面进行组织。

Method: 引入“图工程”这一新兴范式。不同于以往主要优化个体交互或代理层行为的方法，该方法构建显式、动态且不断演化的图结构，以表示任务、智能体和系统状态。这些抽象概念为组织复杂目标、编排异构智能体、建模系统动力学以及支持可扩展的智能体演化提供了统一基础。

Result: 提出了“系统智能”（System Intelligence）概念，即智能体系统协调多个组件以实现共同目标的能力。通过系统性地审查图工程的原则、方法论及应用，证明了其在处理复杂、长期任务中的有效性，并建立了相关的资源库（论文、开源数据和项目）。

Conclusion: 未来的LLM智能体系统必须从优化单体转向系统级协作。图工程作为下一代智能体系统的基础范式，通过显式的图结构解决了复杂任务的组织和协调难题，是实现高效、自适应多智能体系统的关键路径。

Abstract: LLMs have evolved from language generators to autonomous agents capable of complex, long-horizon tasks. This evolution has produced paradigms including Prompt Engineering to elicit model capabilities, Context Engineering to manage information access, Harness Engineering to organize external tools and resources, and Loop Engineering to support continual reflection and self-improvement. Yet as tasks grow more complex, individual intelligence faces a fundamental limit: many tasks require heterogeneous expertise, interdependent subtasks, parallel execution, independent verification, and persistent state, exceeding any single agent's organizational capacity. Augmenting one agent's capabilities or context cannot resolve this architectural mismatch; intelligence must instead be distributed across specialized agents and organized at the system level. We call this System Intelligence: an agent system's ability to organize and coordinate multiple intelligent components into a coherent, adaptive whole pursuing a shared objective. Achieving it requires more than adding agents; it demands explicit structures to organize work, coordinate heterogeneous agents, and maintain evolving execution states. We introduce Graph Engineering, an emerging paradigm for next-generation agent systems. Unlike prior paradigms that mainly optimize individual interactions or agent-level behavior, Graph Engineering constructs explicit, dynamic, evolving graph structures representing tasks, agents, and system states. These abstractions provide a unified foundation for organizing complex objectives, orchestrating heterogeneous agents, modeling system dynamics, and enabling scalable agent evolution. We systematically review the principles, methodologies, and applications of Graph Engineering for LLM agents. Related papers, open-source data, and projects are collected at https://github.com/DEEP-JLU/Awesome-Graph-Engineering.

</details>


### [161] [Adapting Knowledge Graphs for Behavior Denoising in Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21243)
*Zichun Jin,Zihan Zhou,Yinan Liu,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AdaptedKG方法，利用知识图谱（KG）为序列推荐中的每个训练样本生成校准的噪声证据，通过离线计算保留系数来过滤不可靠交互，从而提升推荐性能且不影响推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪方法主要依赖共现、顺序或模型预测判断交互的有效性，缺乏基于项目间关系的显式证据；而直接利用知识图谱又面临流行度偏差、图结构不均等问题，导致可靠性估计失真。

Method: 提出AdaptedKG框架：1. 将观测上下文与结构匹配的替代方案比较，识别异常突出的关系路径以构建局部KG视图；2. 将每个交互与结构匹配参考项比较，在该视图中校准其支持度；3. 利用生成的保留系数门控历史表示并重加权目标损失。所有分数离线计算，不改变骨干网络结构。

Result: 在标准序列推荐器及多种行为去噪序列推荐器上均取得了性能提升，证明了该方法有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: AdaptedKG能够无需修改推荐模型结构即可利用知识图谱提供可靠的去噪信号，解决了传统KG辅助推荐中连接性偏差问题，显著优化了序列推荐效果。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation predicts the next item from a user's interaction history, but not every interaction is equally informative. Real logs combine persistent preferences with temporary needs, exploration, and incidental behavior, so some interactions can distort history representations or provide unreliable supervision. Existing denoising methods judge such interactions mainly from co-occurrence, order, or model predictions, without explicit evidence from relations between items. Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer this evidence, but item popularity, graph degree, uneven coverage, and widely shared entities can inflate connectivity and bias reliability estimates. Here we present AdaptedKG, which derives calibrated KG evidence for each training example without adding graph representations to the recommendation model. It first compares the observed context with structurally matched alternatives to identify relational paths that are unusually prominent and uses them to build a local KG view. It then compares each interaction with structurally matched reference items to calibrate its support within that view. The resulting retention coefficients gate historical representations and reweight target losses. All sample-specific scores are computed offline using training interactions and a fixed KG, so the backbone remains unchanged and no KG access is required at inference. Experiments show gains with a standard sequential recommender and multiple behavior-denoising sequential recommenders.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Ghost Echoes: Semantic Erasure Failure in Retrieval-Backed Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20352)
*Allan Mukkuzhi,Noella Uwayisenga,Yeabsira Bizualem,Jacob Kammerzell,Chandranil Chakraborttii*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文揭示了向量数据库API可见删除与语义彻底擦除之间的差距。通过“幽灵回声”攻击框架，证明已删除记录仍会扰动检索上下文（RAG漂移），且常规全索引重建无法消除此影响，呼吁开发针对检索拓扑的擦除原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管向量数据库正确实现了API层面的数据删除，但这并不保证在基于检索的应用中实现真正的语义擦除。需要验证删除操作是否真正消除了被删数据对下游检索结果的潜在残留影响。

Method: 提出“Ghost Echoes”黑盒攻击框架，利用“同簇非目标删除控制”来近似不可观测的未插入反事实状态，从而隔离并测量目标删除对局部邻域结构的具体影响。在ChromaDB和FAISS等后端上进行评估，对比目标删除与基线的检索中心漂移。

Result: 目标删除导致中位数检索中心漂移为0.1522，在54组配对比较中有53组超过基线（p < 0.001）。即使在查询预算q=5时，信号仍以61.1%的准确率可检测。测试的全索引重建等缓解措施未能消除该漂移。

Conclusion: 存在一个介于接口级删除合规性与真实语义擦除之间的“验证缺口”。现有的仅针对存储标识符的删除机制不足够，需要设计作用于系统检索拓扑的擦除原语，以实现真正的语义擦除。

Abstract: Although vector databases correctly implement API-visible deletion, this does not guarantee complete semantic erasure for retrieval-backed applications. We present Ghost Echoes, a black-box attack framework showing that deleted records can leave measurable residual influence on downstream retrieval contexts. Our primary finding is the RAG retrieval-context drift effect where even when a target record is correctly excluded from query results, its prior presence perturbs the semantic centroid and textual composition of the Top-K evidence base. We approximate the unobservable never-inserted counterfactual using a same-cluster non-target deletion control that preserves local neighborhood structure while isolating target-specific effects from generic local drift. Evaluation on ChromaDB confirms target deletion produces a median retrieval-centroid drift of 0.1522, exceeding the same-cluster baseline in 53/54 paired comparisons (p < 0.001), and the signal remains detectable with 61.1% accuracy at a query budget of q = 5. We verify API-visible deletion correctness across evaluated backends and observe the same qualitative drift ordering in matched FAISS replication. Under the evaluated settings, tested operational mitigations such as full index rebuilds fail to eliminate the measured drift. These results establish a measurable 'verification gap' between interface-level deletion compliance and true semantic erasure, and motivate erasure primitives that act not only on stored identifiers, but also on the retrieval topology of the system.

</details>


### [163] [Disentangling Structure and Semantics: How Schema Representation Affects LLM-Based SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20356)
*Daniel Yitian Su,Sophie Yiran Su,Qiang Sun,Yihao Ding,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 研究揭示在LLM驱动的Text-to-SQL任务中，语义线索（表/列名）比结构线索（数据库范式）更重要；富有意义的名称可以弥补结构缺失，但晦涩的名称无法通过丰富的结构元数据来补救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常孤立地考察数据库的结构线索和语义线索，缺乏对两者相对重要性及相互替代关系的系统性比较。

Method: 设计了6x3因子实验，交叉测试6个结构层级（L1-L6，从宽表到3NF）与3个语义层级（S1-S3，匿名、缩写、描述性标识符），在397个修正的BIRD问题上评估了9种不同规模的模型（0.5B至旗舰级）。

Result: 发现两个轴之间存在非对称替代效应：有意义的名称能补偿结构缺失，但晦涩名称下丰富结构无法恢复性能。这种效应在8/9数据库中复现，且随模型规模增大而显著（3B以下模型影响可忽略）。

Conclusion: 对于当前基于LLM的Text-to-SQL，主要瓶颈在于语义接地（Semantic Grounding）而非关系暴露（Relational Exposure），即清晰的数据命名比复杂的数据库范式结构更为关键。

Abstract: LLM-based text-to-SQL pipelines read the database schema as text, which carries both structural cues (tables, keys, relationships) and semantic cues (table and column names); prior work has studied each axis in isolation, leaving open how they compare in magnitude and whether they substitute for one another. We present a controlled 6 times 3 factorial design crossing structural levels L_1--L_6 (from a denormalised wide table to a 3NF schema with foreign keys and explicit join paths) with semantic levels S_1--S_3 (anonymous, abbreviated, descriptive identifiers), evaluated on 397 corrected BIRD questions with identical gold queries throughout; we materialise 1NF and 2NF variants for nine BIRD databases to support the lowest structural levels. Across nine models from 0.5B to flagship scale we find an asymmetric substitution between the two axes, meaningful names compensate for missing structure but richer structural metadata does not recover performance when names are opaque, which reproduces in 8 of 9 databases and emerges with model scale (negligible below 3B). Within the structural axis the dominant lever is normalisation itself, not metadata layered on top of 3NF, suggesting that for current LLM-based text-to-SQL the practical bottleneck is semantic grounding rather than relational exposure.

</details>


### [164] [bikiDATA: A Python Library to Query and Explore Large-Scale RDF Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20358)
*Etienne Posthumus,Sven Hertling,Dilek Yargan,Harald Sack*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: bikiDATA 是一个面向现代 Python 开发者的知识图谱存储解决方案及库，旨在通过提供原生风格的 API 和集成搜索、嵌入等功能，降低 RDF 数据的使用门槛并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 RDF 三元组与软件工程师使用的原生对象之间的语义鸿沟，消除传统知识图谱应用开发中对 SPARQL 和复杂数据映射层的深度依赖，从而降低使用门槛。

Method: 开发名为 bikiDATA 的高性能存储解决方案和 Python 库。该系统抽象了 RDF 数据模型的复杂性，提供符合 Python 生态习惯的开发者友好型 API，并集成全文搜索、知识图谱嵌入和视觉相似度搜索等生产级功能。

Result: bikiDATA 已在 FIZ Karlsruhe 的持续项目中使用，成功降低了集成复杂度，提高了可扩展性，并增强了查询性能。其源代码和可执行演示笔记本已公开。

Conclusion: bikiDATA 通过简化接口和增强功能，有效弥合了知识图谱技术与现代软件开发之间的差距，为构建高性能、易用的知识图谱应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While knowledge graphs offer unparalleled data flexibility, the semantic gap between RDF triples and the native objects used by software engineers remains a significant barrier to entry. Developing knowledge-graph-backed applications typically requires deep expertise in SPARQL and complex data-mapping layers. To lower this threshold, we present bikiDATA: a high-performance storage solution and a Python library engineered for the modern software developer. Unlike traditional wrappers, bikiDATA abstracts the complexities of the RDF data model into a developer-friendly API that feels native to the Python ecosystem. Beyond standard SPARQL support, the system provides a comprehensive suite for production-grade applications, including integrated full-text search, knowledge graph embeddings, and visual similarity search. Already in use in ongoing projects at FIZ Karlsruhe, bikiDATA reduces integration complexity, improves scalability, and enhances query performance. The source code and executable demo notebook are publicly available at https://github.com/ISE-FIZKarlsruhe/bikidata.

</details>


### [165] [Bolo: Verified Model Hub for Next-Generation AI Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20525)
*Yunqi Li,Ila Petrovic,Yongjoo Park*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为\system的多阶段智能体平台，通过自动修复和合成推理管道，将Hugging Face等平台上大量不可用的模型权重转化为经过验证的、可直接使用的推理流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI模型平台（如Hugging Face）虽拥有海量模型仓库，但许多仅包含原始权重而缺乏可运行的推理管道，常因依赖缺失、模型类不支持或任务分配错误等原因失败。人工构建大规模已验证模型库几乎不可能，亟需自动化解决方案以支持多模态数据库的高精度与高效率需求。

Method: 开发了一个由多阶段智能体系统驱动的平台\system：1) 对于标准用法下失败的模型，智能体检查错误并修复损坏的管道（Type I）；2) 对于超出现有接口范围的模型，智能体利用元数据和文档从头合成管道（Type II & III）；3) 实施多阶段验证机制，不仅检查程序结构，还验证语义行为，确保输出有意义而非仅执行无误。

Result: 初步实验显示，\system在Type II和Type III模型上分别实现了97.27%和86.08%的可运行覆盖率，证明基于智能体的合成与针对性验证能将大量不可用模型权重转化为经过验证的推理管道数据库。该初步数据库已在https://bolobao.ai/开源。

Conclusion: 智能体技术能够规模化地解决模型可用性难题，通过将不可用的模型权重转化为经过严格验证的推理流水线，为未来AI数据库提供了关键基础设施。

Abstract: Verified, ready-to-use inference pipelines are a cornerstone of future AI databases. They allow multi-modal databases to incorporate specialized language, vision, and tabular models that can deliver both high accuracy and efficiency. Unfortunately, existing model platforms such as Hugging Face fall short of this goal. While they host millions of model repositories, many contain only raw weights without runnable pipelines. Even well-documented models often fail due to missing dependencies, unsupported model classes, or incorrect task assignments. Moreover, different models fail for different reasons, with no uniform solution. Constructing a large-scale, verified model hub is nearly impossible with human effort alone.
  We argue that AI agents can achieve this at scale. We present \system, a model platform that hosts verified, ready-to-use inference pipelines, powered by a multi-stage agentic system for model remediation. For models that fail under standard usage, the agent inspects errors and repairs broken pipelines (Type~I). For models outside the scope of existing interfaces, it synthesizes pipelines from scratch using model metadata and documentation (Type~II \& III). To prevent incorrect pipelines from entering the database, the agent applies multi-stage verification---checking not only program structure but also semantic model behavior, ensuring pipelines produce meaningful outputs rather than merely executing without error. In preliminary experiments, \system achieves 97.27\% and 86.08\% runnable coverage for Type~II and Type~III models, respectively, demonstrating that agentic synthesis with targeted verification can transform large collections of unusable model weights into a verified database of ready-to-use inference pipelines. The preliminary database is open-sourced at \textcolor{blue}{https://bolobao.ai/}.

</details>


### [166] [Improving Join Order Optimization on Gate-Based Quantum Computers via Structured Parameter Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20683)
*Divya Shekar,Ruokun Wu,Dhanvi Bharadwaj,Gokul Subramanian Ravi,Lin Ma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出使用SPIQ（Scalable Parameter Initialization for QAOA）作为初始化策略，结合量子近似优化算法（QAOA）解决关系查询优化中的连接顺序优化（JOO）问题。通过在3和4个关系的小规模问题上进行模拟评估，结果表明结构化初始化能显著提高收敛速度和最优解采样频率（最高提升5倍），优于随机初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于门控量子设备的连接顺序优化方法存在可扩展性限制，且缺乏对初始化策略在提升门控量子优化性能方面作用的研究。旨在探索更有效的初始化方法以改善量子优化在数据库工作负载中的表现。

Method: 将连接顺序优化问题重构为二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）问题，采用量子近似优化算法（QAOA）进行求解，并引入SPIQ策略来识别高质量的初始参数点。通过模拟评估QUBO编码、SPIQ初始化与门控优化在小规模连接排序问题（涉及3和4个关系）中的相互作用。

Result: 结构化初始化提高了优化的稳定性，并促使算法向高质量连接计划收敛。在模拟实例中，SPIQ使最优连接顺序的采样频率提高约5倍，最终状态能量显著低于随机初始化的QAOA。

Conclusion: 该工作增强了现有的门控量子优化技术，并为将SPIQ初始化应用于数据库查询优化工作负载提供了初步的概念验证。

Abstract: Join Order Optimization (JOO) is one of the most computationally expensive tasks in relational query optimization due to the exponential growth of possible join plans with increasing query size. Recent work has explored quantum and quantum-inspired approaches for solving JOO by reformulating the problem as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) problem suitable for optimization using quantum hardware. However, many existing approaches have limited scalability on current gate-based quantum devices. In addition, little work has investigated the role of initialization strategies in improving the performance of gate-based quantum optimization for database workloads. In this work, we investigate gate-based quantum join order optimization using the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) initialized with Scalable Parameter Initialization for QAOA (SPIQ). SPIQ is used to efficiently identify high-quality initial points in the quantum solution landscape for QAOA executed on a gate-based quantum computer. We evaluate the interaction between QUBO encoding, SPIQ initialization, and gate-based optimization on small-scale join ordering problems involving 3 and 4 relations. Our results show that structured initialization improves optimization stability and increases convergence toward high-quality join plans compared to uninformed initialization approaches. Across these small-scale, simulation-based instances, SPIQ increases the sampling frequency of the optimal join order by up to approximately 5$\times$ and yields final-state energies significantly lower than a randomly initialized QAOA. Overall, this work enhances existing gate-based quantum optimization while providing an initial proof of concept for applying SPIQ initialization to database query optimization workloads.

</details>


### [167] [Integrating Semantics into Research Data Management: Modelling and Validating Materials Science Experiment Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20879)
*Samuel García Vázquez,Victor Dudarev,Alfred Ludwig,Markus Stricker,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了如何将关系型科研数据管理系统（RDM）建模为本体，并构建知识图谱（KG）。通过利用数据的互联性，不仅实现了高效KG的构建，还通过生成SHACL形状来验证实验工作流的合规性，最终集成到用户界面中，支持研究人员规划、复用、共享和跟踪实验进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语义网技术和本体在科研数据管理（RDM）中日益普及，但如何有效利用知识图谱（KG）整合异构数据源，并利用其 expressive SPARQL 查询和 SHACL 复杂数据验证规则等优势，以支持更高级的应用场景，仍需进一步探索。

Method: 1. 将关系型 RDM 系统建模为本体；2. 基于该本体构建知识图谱（KG）；3. 对 KG 中记录的实验工作流建立理想对应模型；4. 基于理想模型编程生成 SHACL 形状，以全面验证实验工作流的合规性；5. 将上述功能集成到用户界面（UI）中。

Result: 成功从特定领域的 RDM 系统中高效构建了知识图谱；实现了通过 SHACL 形状对实验工作流进行自动化且全面的合规性验证；研究人员能够通过 UI 界面方便地规划、复用、共享及跟踪日常实验进展。

Conclusion: 利用语义网技术和知识图谱可以显著提升科研数据管理的互操作性和智能化水平，特别是通过结合本体建模与 SHACL 验证机制，能够有效支持复杂的科研工作流程管理与质量控制。

Abstract: The incorporation of Semantic Web technologies within scientific environments is becoming an increasingly popular Research Data Management (RDM) practice. While ontologies offer flexible, reusable and machine-readable vocabularies to describe domain-specific research data, Knowledge Graphs (KGs) facilitate the integration of heterogeneous data sources into an interoperable collection. Furthermore, KGs offer additional advantages, notably the use of expressive SPARQL queries, or the ability to define complex data validation rules with SHACL. This work describes the modelling of a relational RDM system with an ontology, and the subsequent construction of a KG based on it. Enabled by the highly interconnected nature of research data and experiment workflows present in the system, we not only show how we can easily and reliably build an efficient KG from such domain-specific RDM systems, but also how doing so enables more advanced use cases. This is demonstrated by the modelling of ideal counterparts for the experiment workflows logged in the KG, which are then used to programmatically generate SHACL shapes that fully validate the conformance of the latter. By integrating this functionality within a UI, we allow researchers to plan, reuse, share, and track the progress of their daily experiments.

</details>


### [168] [Causal Explanations for Stratified Datalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21141)
*Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了安全分层Datalog逻辑下基于规则的推理中异常处理的原因分析。通过引入干预和完美模型语义，证明了最小支持不能单独决定因果关系，并提出了基于兼容素蕴涵项的最小伴随条件大小特征化方法。在固定非递归分层程序下，确定了原因识别、鲁棒性和责任的NP完全性，以及干预-响应等价性的coNP完全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含否定（特别是分层否定）的Datalog程序中，单调性被破坏，导致传统的基于最小支持的方法无法准确确定删除因果关系。因为插入或删除事实可能会创建或销毁答案，需要新的方法来处理这种非单调性下的因果解释。

Method: 研究安全且分层的Datalog在完美模型语义下的实际原因、责任和鲁棒性。采用对有限集可变外延事实的干预方法。通过分析最小支持和改变结果的最小干预之间的关系，利用兼容素蕴涵项来表征观察结果和相反结果的最小伴随条件。

Result: 1. 证明最小支持和最小干预不能完全决定因果关系；2. 发现鲁棒性半径为一时可能伴随无界的最小伴随条件；3. 提出了基于兼容素蕴涵项的最小伴随条件大小特征化方法；4. 该方法保守地恢复了正Datalog的支持基础因果性，并为阻塞递归可达性提供了路径-割特征化；5. 确立了固定非递归分层程序下相关问题的计算复杂性界限。

Conclusion: 对于包含分层否定的Datalog程序，传统的单调性方法不再适用。本文提出的基于兼容素蕴涵项的特征化方法为处理此类非单调逻辑中的因果关系提供了理论基础，并明确了不同因果属性问题的计算复杂度。

Abstract: Rule-based reasoning with exceptions requires causal explanations that account for both present and absent facts. For positive Datalog, monotonicity allows minimal supports to determine deletion causality. Stratified negation removes that property because inserting or deleting a fact may create or destroy an answer. We study actual causes, responsibility, and robustness for safe and stratified Datalog under perfect-model semantics and interventions over a finite set of mutable extensional facts. We prove that minimal supports and inclusion-minimal outcome-changing interventions do not determine causality, while robustness radius one may coexist with unbounded minimum contingencies. Our main result characterizes the minimum contingency size of a candidate fact by compatible prime implicants for the observed and opposite outcomes. The characterization conservatively recovers support-based causality for positive Datalog and yields path--cut characterizations for blocked recursive reachability. For fixed nonrecursive stratified programs, we establish data-complexity NP-completeness for cause recognition, robustness, and responsibility, and coNP-completeness for intervention-response equivalence between two such programs.

</details>


### [169] [Indexing Long Documents for LLM-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21237)
*Donna Pham*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于层次化纯文本索引的长文档分析方法，通过LLM构建一次性索引以加速后续查询，在NarrativeQA数据集上实现了与最强基线DocETL相当的准确率，且成本降低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接为每个问题输入完整长文档（如临床记录、法律合同）给大语言模型会导致速度慢、成本高、易产生幻觉，且无法复用之前的计算工作。

Method: 受B+树启发但针对LLM访问优化的层次化纯文本索引：1) 内容存在于每一层级；2) 索引为纯文本而非属性值；3) 导航基于页面摘要的相关性而非搜索键比较。结构由LLM针对每个文档自动发现，无需人工设计。

Result: 在NarrativeQA数据集上的初步评估显示，该索引方法的准确性与最强基线DocETL相当，但回答问题的成本降低了40%。

Conclusion: 这种分层索引方法提供了一种高效、低成本的大语言模型长文档分析解决方案，优于直接全文输入模式。

Abstract: Long documents such as clinical records, legal contracts, and scientific papers are increasingly analyzed with large language models (LLMs). Naturally, feeding the full document to the model for every question can eventually become slow, expensive, prone to hallucination, and it reuses no work across questions. We explore an indexing-based solution for document analysis and propose a hierarchical plain-text index that is built once per document and consulted by subsequent queries. Inspired by the classic B+ tree, the index organizes a document into pages arranged from general summaries at the root to specific ones at the leaves, but it departs from the B+ tree in three ways suited to LLM access: content lives at every level, the index is plain text rather than attribute values, and navigation follows relevance between page summaries rather than comparing a search key. Its structure is discovered per document by the LLM rather than being hand-designed. In a preliminary evaluation on the NarrativeQA dataset, the index reaches accuracy comparable to DocETL, the strongest baseline, while being 40\% cheaper to answer questions.

</details>


### [170] [VTRQ: Enabling Verifiable Trajectory Range Queries in Hybrid-Storage Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21314)
*Zhongming Yao,Junchang Xin,Yumeng Song,Yusen Mao,Kristian Torp,Yuemin Ding,Divesh Srivastava,Yushuai Li,Christian S. Jensen,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出VTRQ框架，用于混合存储区块链中的可验证轨迹范围查询。通过构建空间ADS（基于路网层级聚合）和时间ADS（基于区间树），实现高效的空间与时间分离验证及聚合，解决现有ADS在多维连续轨迹数据验证上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外包轨迹存储和查询存在服务商返回错误结果的风险，需确保结果可验证性。现有混合存储区块链缺乏支持多维、连续轨迹数据的高效认证数据结构（ADS），一维或离散数据的ADS不适用于此类场景。

Method: 提出VTRQ框架，包含两种高效ADS：(1) 空间ADS：利用分层组织聚合轨迹、边和节点哈希，减少冗余计算；(2) 时间ADS：基于区间树，仅索引起止时间以支持剪枝。通过时空分离索引降低数据比较需求，并提供时空边聚合机制结合时空验证与交集分析实现过滤。

Result: 实现了首个针对混合存储区块链的可验证轨迹范围查询框架，有效解决了多维连续轨迹数据的验证难题，提升了查询和验证效率。

Conclusion: VTRQ通过创新的空间和时间ADS设计及聚合机制，为外包环境下的轨迹数据查询提供了高效、可靠的验证解决方案，弥补了现有技术在多维连续数据验证上的空白。

Abstract: Due to their increasingly large volumes, outsourcing of trajectory storage and querying to third-party service providers has become attractive. However, in such outsourced environments, service providers may return incorrect, e.g., incomplete, tampered, or invalid query results, making verifiability of query results an important consideration. Existing hybrid-storage blockchains offer limited support for trajectory data, lacking authenticated data structures (ADS) that enable efficient verification. For example, ADSs designed for queries on one-dimensional data are unsuitable for queries on multidimensional trajectory data, while ADSs tailored for discrete data may yield incomplete results when applied to continuous trajectory data. We propose the first framework for verifiable trajectory range queries in hybrid-storage blockchains, called VTRQ. It features two efficient ADSs: (i) a spatial ADS for road networks that leverages hierarchical organization to aggregate trajectory, edge, and node hashes, thus reducing redundant computations and improving spatial verification efficiency; and (ii) a temporal ADS based on interval trees, which indexes only the start and end times of trajectories, thereby enabling pruning and efficient temporal verification. By separating spatial and temporal indexing, the method reduces the need for data comparison, enhancing both query and verification efficiency. To aggregate spatial and temporal query results, VTRQ provides a spatio-temporal edge aggregation mechanism that combines temporal verification of spatial nodes, spatial intersection computation, and temporal intersection analysis to achieve spatio-temporal filtering.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“规范驱动的智能体开发”（SDAD）范式，主张利用大语言模型处理长上下文的能力，将软件工程的重心从编码阶段前置到规范制定阶段，通过高精度的机器可读规范实现自动化开发与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着拥有超长上下文窗口的大语言模型的发展，软件开发生命周期（SDLC）正在被重构。当前的痛点在于如何利用AI进行大规模需求文档和代码库的处理，以及如何平衡敏捷开发的灵活性与工程纪律的严谨性。作者旨在确立一种新的生产范式，以解决自主交付中的规范质量与执行效率问题。

Method: 1. 定义SDAD流程：包括意图捕获、机器可读规范生成、智能体合成及独立多智能体验证，最终由人类签署确认。
2. 历史对比分析：回顾瀑布模型与敏捷开发的演变，引入“AI代码”作为第四种生产范式，对比“人类-敏捷”（约2020年）与“智能体-SDAD”（约2026年）在工件、节奏、问责制和安全态势上的差异。
3. 量化治理模型：扩展团队角色转变（工程师、QA、平台、产品职能），并建立量化指标体系，包括模糊税（Ambiguity Tax）、规范保真度（Spec Fidelity）、系统错误率（SER）及带有修复乘数phi的TCI_agentic。
4. 混合估算与分阶段迁移蓝图：提供务实的采用策略。

Result: 研究证实了分离“合成”与“发布权限”的重要性，整合了AI增强测试与验证的工业与研究证据。研究表明，智能体的速度并未消除工程纪律，而是将其重新定位到上游的规范精确性、明确的检查点和可审计的溯源性中。

Conclusion: 未来的软件开发将由高精度规范驱动，智能体负责快速实现，而工程纪律的核心转移至前期的规范制定与验证环节，形成一种结合形式化方法与高速实施的新范式。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [172] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAO系统，通过并行查询PostgreSQL和Cloudflare Worker两个记忆后端，将用户历史数据编译为简报并注入Claude Code代理，解决LLM编码会话上下文丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）编码代理每次启动时上下文窗口为空，导致先前工作的累积知识被丢弃。

Method: PAO系统在生成新实例时，并行查询实体-观察数据库和语义搜索索引，使用特定策略融合结果，并通过文件系统注入利用主机代理的配置自动读取行为来交付简报；同时管理信任预播种、就绪轮询及自适应终端文本注入等完整生命周期。

Result: 报告了2025年12月至2026年3月期间四个月定期部署的经验，记录了三种上下文交付机制的迭代、导致重新设计的具体失败模式，以及桥接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统的工程权衡。

Conclusion: 该经验报告证明了通过桥接异构记忆系统并利用现有代理特性进行上下文注入的有效性，强调了在工程实现中处理失败模式和系统权衡的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [173] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大语言模型（LLM）安全对齐的脆弱性，提出了“意图地平线”概念，并设计了基于早期层特征检测的轻量级防御机制LIV，有效抵御语义伪装攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐往往流于表面，仅在生成末尾触发拒绝机制，无法消除预训练阶段习得的有害知识概念，导致模型易受“语义伪装”对抗攻击的影响，即通过 benign 叙事包裹有害意图以绕过护栏。

Method: 分析Phi-3、Qwen2.5和Gemma-2b三种小型语言模型在对抗压力下的潜在激活轨迹；提出“意图地平线”概念，识别出有害意图表征崩溃的关键深度（通常为总层数的15-20%）；设计Latent Intent Verification (LIV) 轻量级探测防御机制，利用早期层中保留的可检测“有害签名”进行验证。

Result: 发现晚期层表征中伪装攻击与正常查询数学上不可区分（检测率<20%），但早期层表征保留了独特的可检测“有害签名”。在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上的实验表明，LIV在所有测试架构上比标准护栏高出20-50%的性能，能有效中和零日语义攻击且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘模型内部早期层的潜在表征差异，可以有效解决当前LLM安全对齐中存在的深层漏洞，提供一种无需重训的高效防御方案，显著提升了模型对复杂语义对抗攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [174] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了多模态大模型（LMMs）如何增强智能体（Agent）的感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动能力，填补了现有研究对多模态在智能体中作用缺乏系统分析的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在针对基于LLM的智能体的调查，但近年来缺乏对多模态性如何塑造智能体能力的系统性考察。

Method: 通过以模态为中心的分类法，分析多模态在智能体核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）中的影响；梳理从文本中心到多模态框架的演变；评估委托、晚期融合和早期融合架构；并在机器人、GUI/Web导航等领域审查应用性能及效率-可扩展性权衡。

Result: 揭示了多模态集成架构与智能体能力之间的联系，分析了不同应用场景下的性能表现，并讨论了训练/推理成本、延迟和部署约束等效率问题。

Conclusion: 旨在识别当前关键差距，并为构建鲁棒且通用的智能系统绘制发展路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [175] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于树集成（LDT/LDF/LDAB）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，旨在平衡Transformer的高精度与可解释性不足的问题。通过概念聚类降维和修改后的特征重要性度量，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时，显著提高了对人类标注者的一致性，解决了临床和教育等信任敏感应用中的黑盒问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高容量模型（如Transformer）虽然预测性能强劲，但其分布式表示和深度非线性使得难以赋予多模态特征有意义的权重，限制了其在需要人类可理解解释的信任敏感应用（如临床情感监测、教育评估）中的使用。当前模型无法同时实现具有竞争力的准确性和产生人类可理解的决策依据解释这一双重目标。

Method: 开发了一个基于树集成的框架：1) 将每种模态编码为token；2) 提取并聚类概念以降低维度；3) 通过树基集成分类器路由融合的多模态数据；4) 使用一种新颖的修改后特征重要性指标进行解释。该指标减少了二元分类任务中负类的影响，从而改善指标或标记检测。提出的具体模型包括线性判别树（LDT）、线性判别森林（LDF）和线性判别AdaBoost（LDAB）。

Result: 1) 性能提升：F1-mod得分比多模态Transformer提高4.3%，准确率比主要可解释基线（IMR）提高3.0%。2) 可解释性提升：所提出的多模态特征重要性在IEMOCAP数据集上的人类标注者一致性得分为62.2%（优于默认的43.2%），在CMU-MOSI数据集上为46.7%（优于默认的32.1%），能更有效地提取显著的多模态概念。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个兼顾高精度和高可解释性的多模态分析框架，通过改进的特征重要性度量显著增强了人类对模型决策依据的信任和理解，为临床和教育领域的可信AI应用提供了有效解决方案。
